          <p>For property classified as an agricultural homestead under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13#stat.273.13.23">273.13, subdivision 23</a>, paragraph (a), ownership percentages for property owned by tenants in common are based on deeded ownership amounts for each owner who homesteads the property.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1245">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1245 CLASSIFICATION OF DATA.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1245.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Private or nonpublic data.</span></h2>
          <p>The following data are private or nonpublic data as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02">13.02, subdivisions 9</a> and 12, when they are submitted to a county or local assessor under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.124">273.124</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>, or another section, to support a claim for the property tax homestead classification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>, or other property tax classification or benefit:</p>
          <p>(1) Social Security numbers;</p>
          <p>(2) individual taxpayer identification numbers;</p>
          <p>(3) copies of state or federal income tax returns; and</p>
          <p>(4) state or federal income tax return information, including the federal income tax schedule F.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1245.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Disclosure.</span></h2>
          <p>The assessor shall disclose the data described in subdivision 1 to the commissioner of revenue as provided by law. The assessor shall also disclose all or portions of the data described in subdivision 1 to:</p>
          <p>(1) the county treasurer solely for the purpose of proceeding under the Revenue Recapture Act to recover personal property taxes owing; and</p>
          <p>(2) the county veterans service officer for the purpose of determining a person's eligibility for the veteran with a disability homestead market value exclusion under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>, subdivision 34.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1245.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/82/#laws.0.21.0">2013 c 82 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.4.14.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 4 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/10/#laws.6.6.0">1Sp2019 c 10 art 6 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/64/#laws.3.14.0">2023 c 64 art 3 s 14</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.125">
        <h1 class="shn">273.125 ASSESSMENT OF MANUFACTURED HOMES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.125.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Valuation; notice.</span></h2>
          <p>Subdivisions 1 to 7 apply to manufactured homes that are assessed under subdivision 8, paragraph (c). Each manufactured home must be valued each year by the assessor and assessed with reference to its value on January 2 of that year. Notice of the value must be mailed to the person to be assessed at least ten days before the meeting of the local board of review or equalization. The notice must contain the amount of valuation in terms of market value, the assessor's office address, and the date, place, and time set for the meeting of the local board of review or equalization and the county board of equalization.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.125.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Return assessment books; set tax.</span></h2>
          <p>On or before May 1, the assessor shall return to the county auditor the assessment books relating to the assessment of manufactured homes. After receiving the assessment books, the county auditor shall determine the tax to be due by applying the rate of levy of the preceding year and shall send a list of the taxes to the county treasurer by May 30.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.125.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Tax statements; penalties; collections.</span></h2>
          <p>Not later than July 15 in the year of assessment the county treasurer shall mail to the taxpayer a statement of tax due on a manufactured home. The taxes are due on the last day of August, or 20 days after the postmark date on the envelope containing the property tax statement, whichever is later, except that if the tax exceeds $50, one-half of the amount due may be paid on August 31, or 20 days after the postmark date on the envelope containing the property tax statement, whichever is later, and the remainder on November 15. Taxes remaining unpaid after the due date are delinquent, and a penalty of eight percent must be assessed and collected as part of the unpaid taxes. The tax statement must contain a sentence notifying the taxpayer that the title to the manufactured home cannot be transferred unless the property taxes are paid.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.125.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Petitions of grievance.</span></h2>
          <p>A person who claims that the person's manufactured home has been unfairly or unequally assessed, or that the property has been assessed at a valuation greater than its real or actual value, or that the tax levied against it is illegal, in whole or in part, or has been paid, or that the property is exempt from the tax so levied, may have the validity of the claim, defense, or objection determined in court. The determination must be made by the district court of the county in which the tax is levied or by the Tax Court. A person can request the determination by filing a petition for it in the office of the court administrator of the district court on or before October 1 of the year in which the tax becomes payable. A petition for determination under this section may be transferred by the district court to the Tax Court.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.125.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Continuing with petition.</span></h2>
          <p>The right to continue prosecution of the petition is conditioned upon the payment of the tax when due unless the court permits the petitioner to continue without payment, or with a reduced payment, under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/278.03#stat.278.03.2">278.03, subdivision 2</a>. Upon ten days' notice to the county attorney and to the county auditor, given at least ten days before the last day of August, the petitioner may apply to the court for permission to continue prosecution of the petition without payment or with a reduced payment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.125.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Correcting tax.</span></h2>
          <p>If the local board of review or equalization or the county board of equalization changes the assessor's valuation of a manufactured home, the change must be sent to the county auditor. The auditor shall immediately recompute the tax and advise the treasurer of the corrected tax. If the property is entitled to homestead classification, the auditor shall reduce the tax accordingly.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.125.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Personal property.</span></h2>
          <p>The tax assessed on manufactured homes is a personal property tax. Laws relating to assessment, review, and collection of personal property taxes apply to this tax, if consistent with this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.125.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Manufactured homes; sectional structures.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) In this section, "manufactured home" means a structure transportable in one or more sections, which is built on a permanent chassis, and designed to be used as a dwelling with or without a permanent foundation when connected to the required utilities, and contains the plumbing, heating, air conditioning, and electrical systems in it. Manufactured home includes any accessory structure that is an addition or supplement to the manufactured home and, when installed, becomes a part of the manufactured home.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Except as provided in paragraph (c), a manufactured home that meets each of the following criteria must be valued and assessed as an improvement to real property, the appropriate real property classification applies, and the valuation is subject to review and the taxes payable in the manner provided for real property:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the title to the land on which it is situated is held by: (i) the owner of the unit; or (ii) a Minnesota nonprofit corporation or a Minnesota cooperative to which the owner is a member;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the unit is affixed to the land by a permanent foundation or is installed at its location in accordance with the Manufactured Home Building Code in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/327.31">327.31 </a>to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/327.34">327.34</a>, and rules adopted under those sections, or is affixed to the land like other real property in the taxing district; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the unit is connected to public utilities, has a well and septic tank system, or is serviced by water and sewer facilities comparable to other real property in the taxing district.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) A manufactured home that meets each of the following criteria must be assessed at the rate provided by the appropriate real property classification but must be treated as personal property, and the valuation is subject to review and the taxes payable in the manner provided in this section:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the owner of the unit is a lessee of the land under the terms of a lease, or the unit is located in a manufactured home park but is not the homestead of the park owner;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the unit is affixed to the land by a permanent foundation or is installed at its location in accordance with the Manufactured Home Building Code contained in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/327.31">327.31</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/327.34">327.34</a>, and the rules adopted under those sections, or is affixed to the land like other real property in the taxing district; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the unit is connected to public utilities, has a well and septic tank system, or is serviced by water and sewer facilities comparable to other real property in the taxing district.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Sectional structures must be valued and assessed as an improvement to real property if the owner of the structure holds title to the land on which it is located or is a qualifying lessee of the land under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.19">273.19</a>. In this paragraph "sectional structure" means a building or structural unit that has been in whole or substantial part manufactured or constructed at an off-site location to be wholly or partially assembled on site alone or with other units and attached to a permanent foundation.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The commissioner of revenue may adopt rules under the Administrative Procedure Act to establish additional criteria for the classification of manufactured homes and sectional structures under this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) A storage shed, deck, or similar improvement constructed on property that is leased or rented as a site for a manufactured home, sectional structure, park trailer, or travel trailer is taxable as provided in this section. In the case of property that is leased or rented as a site for a travel trailer, a storage shed, deck, or similar improvement on the site that is considered personal property under this paragraph is taxable only if its total estimated market value is over $10,000. The property is taxable as personal property to the lessee of the site if it is not owned by the owner of the site. The property is taxable as real estate if it is owned by the owner of the site. As a condition of permitting the owner of the manufactured home, sectional structure, park trailer, or travel trailer to construct improvements on the leased or rented site, the owner of the site must obtain the permanent home address of the lessee or user of the site. The site owner must provide the name and address to the assessor upon request.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.125.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/375/#laws.3.15.0">1993 c 375 art 3 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/490/#laws.5.11.0">2000 c 490 art 5 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/377/#laws.4.15.0">2002 c 377 art 4 s 15</a>; art 9 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/3/#laws.1.13.0">1Sp2005 c 3 art 1 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.2.9.0">2008 c 154 art 2 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.2.9.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 2 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/8/#laws.3.3.0">1Sp2021 c 8 art 3 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.126"><b>273.126</b> MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.96.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 96</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.127"><b>273.127</b> MS 1998 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/490/#laws.5.40.0">2000 c 490 art 5 s 40</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.128">
        <h1 class="shn">273.128 CERTIFICATION OF LOW-INCOME RENTAL PROPERTY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.128.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirement.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Low-income rental property classified as class 4d(1) under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13#stat.273.13.25">273.13, subdivision 25</a>, is entitled to valuation under this section if at least 20 percent of the units in the rental housing property meet any of the following qualifications:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the units are subject to a housing assistance payments contract under Section 8 of the United States Housing Act of 1937, as amended;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the units are rent-restricted and income-restricted units of a qualified low-income housing project receiving tax credits under section 42(g) of the Internal Revenue Code;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the units are financed by the Rural Housing Service of the United States Department of Agriculture and receive payments under the rental assistance program pursuant to section 521(a) of the Housing Act of 1949, as amended; or</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the units are subject to rent and income restrictions under the terms of financial assistance provided to the rental housing property by the federal government or the state of Minnesota, or a local unit of government, as evidenced by a document recorded against the property.</p>
          <p class="in">The restrictions must require assisted units to be occupied by residents whose household income at the time of initial occupancy does not exceed 60 percent of the greater of area or state median income, adjusted for family size, as determined by the United States Department of Housing and Urban Development. The restriction must also require the rents for assisted units to not exceed 30 percent of 60 percent of the greater of area or state median income, adjusted for family size, as determined by the United States Department of Housing and Urban Development.</p>
          <p>(b) The owner of a property certified as class 4d(1) under this section must use the property tax savings received from the 4d(1) classification for one or more of the following eligible uses: property maintenance, property security, improvements to the property, rent stabilization, or increases to the property's replacement reserve account. To maintain the class 4d(1) classification, the property owner must annually reapply and certify to the Housing Finance Agency that the property tax savings were used for one or more eligible uses.</p>
          <p>(c) In order to meet the requirements of this section, property which received the 4d(1) classification in the prior year must demonstrate compliance with paragraph (b).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.128.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Approval.</span></h2>
          <p>A property owner must receive approval by resolution of the governing body of the city or town where the property is located before submitting an initial application to the Housing Finance Agency, as required under subdivision 2, for property that has not, in whole or in part, been classified as class 4d(1) under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>, subdivision 25, prior to assessment year 2024. A property owner that receives approval as required under this subdivision, and the certification made under subdivision 3, shall not be required to seek approval under this subdivision prior to submitting an application under subdivision 2 in each subsequent year. If the property is located in a city or town in which the net tax capacity of 4d(1) property did not exceed two percent of the total net tax capacity in the city or town in the prior assessment year, the property owner does not need to receive approval under this subdivision. The commissioner of revenue must annually certify to the Housing Finance Agency a list of the cities and towns in which the net tax capacity of 4d(1) property exceeded two percent of the total net tax capacity in the prior assessment year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.128.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Application for certification under this section must be filed by March 31 of the levy year, or at a later date if the Housing Finance Agency deems practicable. The application must be filed with the Housing Finance Agency, on a form prescribed by the agency, and must contain the information required by the Housing Finance Agency.</p>
          <p>(b) Each application must include:</p>
          <p>(1) the property tax identification number; and</p>
          <p>(2) evidence that the property meets the requirements of subdivisions 1 and 1a.</p>
          <p>(c) The Housing Finance Agency may charge an application fee approximately equal to the costs of processing and reviewing the applications but not to exceed $10 per unit. If imposed, the applicant must pay the application fee to the Housing Finance Agency. The fee must be deposited in the housing development fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.128.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Certification.</span></h2>
          <p>By June 1 of each levy year, the Housing Finance Agency must certify to the appropriate county or city assessors, the specific properties that are qualified under this section and the number of units in the building that qualify. In making the certification, the Housing Finance Agency may rely on the application and any other supporting information that the agency deems necessary from the property owner.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.128.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/3/#laws.1.14.0">1Sp2005 c 3 art 1 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.2.10.0">2008 c 154 art 2 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/366/#laws.11.12.0">2008 c 366 art 11 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/64/#laws.3.15.0">2023 c 64 art 3 s 15</a>-17</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.13">
        <h1 class="shn">273.13 CLASSIFICATION OF PROPERTY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.13.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">How classified.</span></h2>
          <p>All real and personal property subject to a general property tax and not subject to any gross earnings or other in-lieu tax is hereby classified for purposes of taxation as provided by this section.</p>
        </div>

          <p>MS 1984 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/14/#laws.4.98.0">1Sp1985 c 14 art 4 s 98</a>]</p>
