      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1313"><b>273.1313</b> MS 1986 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.244.0">1987 c 291 s 244</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1314"><b>273.1314</b> MS 1986 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/342/#laws.8.10.0">1983 c 342 art 8 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.244.0">1987 c 291 s 244</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1315">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1315 CERTIFICATION OF CLASS 1B PROPERTY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1315.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Class 1b homestead declaration before 2009.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Any property owner seeking classification and assessment of the owner's homestead as class 1b property pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13#stat.273.13.22">273.13, subdivision 22</a>, paragraph (b), on or before October 1, 2008, shall file with the commissioner of revenue a 1b homestead declaration, on a form prescribed by the commissioner. The declaration shall contain the following information:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the information necessary to verify that on or before June 30 of the filing year, the property owner or the owner's spouse satisfies the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13#stat.273.13.22">273.13, subdivision 22</a>, paragraph (b), for 1b classification; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) any additional information prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="in">The declaration must be filed on or before October 1 to be effective for property taxes payable during the succeeding calendar year. The declaration and any supplementary information received from the property owner pursuant to this subdivision shall be subject to chapter 270B. If approved by the commissioner, the declaration remains in effect until the property no longer qualifies under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13#stat.273.13.22">273.13, subdivision 22</a>, paragraph (b). Failure to notify the commissioner within 30 days that the property no longer qualifies under that paragraph because of a sale, change in occupancy, or change in the status or condition of an occupant shall result in the penalty provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.124">273.124, subdivision</a> 13b, computed on the basis of the class 1b benefits for the property, and the property shall lose its current class 1b classification.</p>
          <p class="in">The commissioner shall provide to the assessor on or before November 1 a listing of the parcels of property qualifying for 1b classification.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1315.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Class 1b homestead declaration 2009 and thereafter.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Any property owner seeking classification and assessment of the owner's homestead as class 1b property pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13#stat.273.13.22">273.13, subdivision 22</a>, paragraph (b), after October 1, 2008, shall file with the county assessor a class 1b homestead declaration, on a form prescribed by the commissioner of revenue. The declaration must contain the following information:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the information necessary to verify that, on or before June 30 of the filing year, the property owner or the owner's spouse satisfies the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>, subdivision 22, paragraph (b), for class 1b classification; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) any additional information prescribed by the commissioner.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The declaration must be filed on or before October 1 to be effective for property taxes payable during the succeeding calendar year. The Social Security numbers, individual taxpayer identification numbers, and income and medical information received from the property owner pursuant to this subdivision are private data on individuals as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02">13.02</a>. If approved by the assessor, the declaration remains in effect until the property no longer qualifies under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13#stat.273.13.22">273.13, subdivision 22</a>, paragraph (b). Failure to notify the assessor within 30 days that the property no longer qualifies under that paragraph because of a sale, change in occupancy, or change in the status or condition of an occupant shall result in the penalty provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.124">273.124, subdivision</a> 13b, computed on the basis of the class 1b benefits for the property, and the property shall lose its current class 1b classification.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1315.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/342/#laws.2.20.0">1983 c 342 art 2 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/522/#laws.0.4.0">1984 c 522 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/14/#laws.4.61.0">1Sp1985 c 14 art 4 s 61</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.20.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 20</a>,82,83; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/426/#laws.1.36.0">1990 c 426 art 1 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.2.15.0">2003 c 127 art 2 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.5.22.0">2005 c 151 art 5 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.2.15.0">2008 c 154 art 2 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/82/#laws.0.22.0">2013 c 82 s 22</a>,23; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/64/#laws.3.21.0">2023 c 64 art 3 s 21</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1316"><b>273.1316</b> MS 1998 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/490/#laws.5.40.0">2000 c 490 art 5 s 40</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1317"><b>273.1317</b> MS 1996 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/231/#laws.1.21.0">1997 c 231 art 1 s 21</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1318"><b>273.1318</b> MS 1996 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/231/#laws.1.21.0">1997 c 231 art 1 s 21</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1319">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1319 SINGLE FAMILY HOUSING; NONCOMPLIANCE; MINNEAPOLIS AND ST. PAUL.</h1>
        <p>(a) If the city determines that a residential rental property classified as class 4bb under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13#stat.273.13.25">273.13, subdivision 25</a>, is not in compliance with the city's applicable rental licensing requirements and housing codes, the city shall notify the property owner of the specific items that are not in compliance. The owner has 60 days to correct the noncompliance items identified by the city. If they have not been corrected within the 60-day time period to the satisfaction of the city, the city shall notify the assessor that the property is out of compliance and is no longer eligible for the class 4bb property classification. Notwithstanding any other provision of law, the assessor shall reclassify the property for the current assessment year, for taxes payable in the following year as class 4b property. The assessor shall notify the property owner of the action.</p>
        <p>(b) This section applies only to property located in the cities of Minneapolis and St. Paul.</p>
        <p>(c) This section is effective for each of the cities of Minneapolis and St. Paul upon compliance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/645.021#stat.645.021.3">645.021, subdivision 3</a>, by the governing body of the city.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1319.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/231/#laws.1.11.0">1997 c 231 art 1 s 11</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>This section was approved by the city of Minneapolis on October 10, 1997, and filed with the Secretary of State on November 14, 1997. This section was not approved by the city of St. Paul.</p>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.132"><b>273.132</b> MS 1988 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.81.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 81</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1321">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1321 VACANT COMMERCIAL INDUSTRIAL PROPERTIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1321.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Authority.</span></h2>
          <p>A city may establish, by ordinance, a program to encourage redevelopment, provide for better utilization of commercial-industrial property, and eliminate blighting influences by revoking the eligibility of individual commercial-industrial properties to receive the credit authorized under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1398#stat.273.1398.4">273.1398, subdivision 4</a>. The program may revoke eligibility of a property only if:</p>
          <p>(1) the property has been vacant, as defined in subdivision 3, clause (1), (2), or (3), for three or more consecutive years prior to the current assessment year; or</p>
          <p>(2) the property has been vacant as defined under subdivision 3, clause (4), for five or more consecutive years prior to the current assessment year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1321.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Minimum program requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>The program must provide:</p>
          <p>(1) standards for determining whether a property is vacant;</p>
          <p>(2) written assessment notice by the city or county to the property owner informing the owner that the property's eligibility will be revoked;</p>
          <p>(3) opportunity for the property owner to appeal the revocation at the local and county board of appeal and equalization;</p>
          <p>(4) timely notice to the county assessor of the property's eligibility revocation, or if the city has a city assessor and the city assessor has revoked the property's eligibility; and</p>
          <p>(5) any other provisions the city determines are necessary or appropriate to the operation of the program to achieve its purposes.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1321.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Definition of vacant.</span></h2>
          <p>A program established under this section may provide that a property is vacant if the property is:</p>
          <p>(1) condemned, dangerous, or having multiple building code violations;</p>
          <p>(2) condemned and illegally occupied;</p>
          <p>(3) either occupied or unoccupied, during which time the enforcement officer for the municipality has issued multiple orders to correct nuisance conditions; or</p>
          <p>(4) unoccupied and not utilized for a commercial or industrial purpose.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1321.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Notice to property owner.</span></h2>
          <p>The municipality shall give notice to the property owner stating that the property may cease to be eligible for the credit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1398#stat.273.1398.4">273.1398, subdivision 4</a>, unless the property is occupied, the conditions in subdivision 3, clauses (1) to (3), are remedied, and the property is used for a commercial or industrial purpose for at least 180 days during the next 12-month period.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1321.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/3/#laws.1.17.0">1Sp2005 c 3 art 1 s 17</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1325">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1325 ADJUSTMENT OF NET TAX CAPACITY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1325.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Computation.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The Department of Revenue must annually conduct an assessment/sales ratio study of the taxable property in each county, city, town, and school district in accordance with the procedures in subdivisions 2 and 3. Based upon the results of this assessment/sales ratio study, the Department of Revenue must determine an equalized net tax capacity for the various classes of taxable property in each taxing district, the aggregate of which is designated as the adjusted net tax capacity. The adjusted net tax capacity must be reduced by the captured tax capacity of tax increment districts under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.177#stat.469.177.2">469.177, subdivision 2</a>, fiscal disparities contribution tax capacities under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/276A.06">276A.06</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473F.08">473F.08</a>, and the tax capacity of transmission lines required to be subtracted from the local tax base under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.425">273.425</a>; and increased by fiscal disparities distribution tax capacities under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/276A.06">276A.06</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473F.08">473F.08</a>. The adjusted net tax capacities shall be determined using the net tax capacity percentages in effect for the assessment year following the assessment year of the study. The Department of Revenue must make whatever estimates are necessary to account for changes in the classification system. The Department of Revenue may incur the expense necessary to make the determinations. The commissioner of revenue may reimburse any county or governmental official for requested services performed in ascertaining the adjusted net tax capacity. On or before March 15 annually, the Department of Revenue shall file with the chair of the Tax Committee of the house of representatives and the chair of the Committee on Taxes and Tax laws of the senate a report of adjusted net tax capacities for school districts. On or before June 30 annually, the Department of Revenue shall file its final report on the adjusted net tax capacities for school districts established by the previous year's assessments and the current year's net tax capacity percentages with the commissioner of education and each county auditor for those school districts for which the auditor has the responsibility for determination of local tax rates. A copy of the report so filed shall be mailed to the clerk of each school district involved and to the county assessor or supervisor of assessments of the county or counties in which each school district is located.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1325.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Methodology.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">In making its annual assessment/sales ratio studies, the Department of Revenue must use a methodology consistent with the most recent Standard on Assessment Ratio Studies published by the assessment standards committee of the International Association of Assessing Officers. The commissioner of revenue shall supplement this general methodology with specific procedures necessary for execution of the study in accordance with other Minnesota laws impacting the assessment/sales ratio study. The commissioner shall document these specific procedures in writing and shall publish the procedures in the State Register, but these procedures will not be considered "rules" pursuant to the Minnesota Administrative Procedure Act. When property is sold and the purchaser changes its use in a manner that would result in a change of classification of the property, the assessment sales ratio study under this subdivision must take into account that changed classification as soon as practicable. A change in status from homestead to nonhomestead or from nonhomestead to homestead is not a change under this subdivision. For purposes of this section, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270.12#stat.270.12.2">270.12, subdivision 2</a>, clause (6), and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/278.05#stat.278.05.4">278.05, subdivision 4</a>, the commissioner of revenue shall exclude from the assessment/sales ratio study the sale of any nonagricultural property which does not contain an improvement, if (1) the statutory basis on which the property's taxable value as most recently assessed is less than market value as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.11">273.11</a>, or (2) the property has undergone significant physical change or a change of use since the most recent assessment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1325.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Agricultural lands.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of determining the adjusted net tax capacity of agricultural lands for the calculation of adjusted net tax capacities, the market value of agricultural lands must be the price for which the property would sell in an arm's-length transaction.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1325.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Forced sales.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of revenue may include forced sales in the assessment/sales ratio studies if it is determined by the commissioner of revenue that these forced sales indicate true market value.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1325.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Stipulated values and abatements.</span></h2>
          <p>The estimated market value to be used in calculating sales ratios must be the value established by the assessor before any stipulations resulting from appeals by property owners and before any abatement unless the abatement was granted for the purpose of correcting mere clerical errors.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1325.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Sales of industrial property.</span></h2>
          <p>Separate sales ratios must be calculated for commercial property and for industrial property. These two classes shall be combined only in jurisdictions in which there is not an adequate sample of sales in each class.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="sr_by_subd" id="stat.273.133">

      <div class="section" id="stat.273.134">
        <h1 class="shn">273.134 TACONITE AND IRON ORE AREAS; TAX RELIEF AREA; DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <p>(a) For purposes of this section and sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.135">273.135</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1391">273.1391</a>, "municipality" means any city, however organized, or town, which meets the following qualifications:</p>
        <p>(1) it is a municipality in which the assessed valuation of unmined iron ore on May 1, 1941, was not less than 40 percent of the assessed valuation of all real property; or</p>
        <p>(2) it is a municipality in which, on January 1, 1977, or the applicable assessment date, there is a taconite concentrating plant or where taconite is mined or quarried or where there is located an electric generating plant which qualifies as a taconite facility.</p>
        <p>"The applicable assessment date" is the date as of which property is listed and assessed for the tax in question.</p>
        <p>(b) For the purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.135">273.135</a>, "tax relief area" means the geographic area contained within the boundaries of a school district which meets the following qualifications:</p>
        <p>(1) it is a school district in which the assessed valuation of unmined iron ore on May 1, 1941, was not less than 40 percent of the assessed valuation of all real property and whose boundaries are within 20 miles of a taconite mine or plant; or</p>
        <p>(2) it is a school district in which, on January 1, 1977, or the applicable assessment date, there is a taconite concentrating plant or where taconite is mined or quarried or where there is located an electric generating plant which qualifies as a taconite facility.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.134.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/1156/#laws.0.4.0">1969 c 1156 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/1/31/#laws.30.8.0">Ex1971 c 31 art 30 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/123/#laws.5.7.0">1973 c 123 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/650/#laws.2.1.0">1973 c 650 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/423/#laws.10.2.0">1977 c 423 art 10 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/416/#laws.1.20.0">1994 c 416 art 1 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.6.4.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 6 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.11.1.0">2003 c 127 art 11 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1341">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1341 TACONITE ASSISTANCE AREA.</h1>
        <p>A "taconite assistance area" means the geographic area that falls within the boundaries of a school district that contains:</p>
        <p>(1) a municipality in which the assessed valuation of unmined iron ore on May 1, 1941, was not less than 40 percent of the assessed valuation of all real property;</p>
        <p>(2) a municipality in which on January 1, 1977, or the applicable assessment date, there is a taconite concentrating plant or where taconite is mined or quarried or where there is located an electric generating plant which qualifies as a taconite facility; or</p>
        <p>(3) a municipality:</p>
        <p>(i) that is located in a county that contains a school district described in clause (1) or (2); and</p>
        <p>(ii) where active mining of materials subject to the tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/298.015">298.015, subdivision </a>1, is occurring, or where a mine subject to the minimum payment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/298.015">298.015</a>, subdivision 3, is located.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1341.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.11.2.0">2003 c 127 art 11 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/21/#laws.11.10.0">1Sp2003 c 21 art 11 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/64/#laws.6.2.0">2023 c 64 art 6 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1342">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1342 ELECTRIC GENERATING PLANTS IN TACONITE TAX RELIEF AREAS.</h1>
        <p class="in">For purposes of definitions of "taconite tax relief area" and "taconite assistance area" in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1341">273.1341</a>, and related laws, the elimination of the property tax exemption for certain electric generating plants under Laws 2008, chapter 154, article 8, section 6, does not change the status of any electric generating plant qualifying as a taconite facility.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1342.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/366/#laws.10.14.0">2008 c 366 art 10 s 14</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.135">
        <h1 class="shn">273.135 HOMESTEAD PROPERTY TAX RELIEF.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.135.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Reduction in tax; tax relief area.</span></h2>
          <p>The property tax to be paid in respect to property taxable within a tax relief area as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (b)</a>, on homestead property, as otherwise determined by law and regardless of the market value of the property, and on nonhomestead portions of property classified as both homestead and nonhomestead property as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.124#stat.273.124.11">273.124, subdivision 11</a>, for all purposes shall be reduced in the amount prescribed by subdivision 2, subject to the limitations contained therein.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.135.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Reduction amount.</span></h2>
          <p>The amount of the reduction authorized by subdivision 1 shall be:</p>
          <p>(a) In the case of property located within a municipality as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (a)</a>, 66 percent of the tax, provided that the reduction shall not exceed the maximum amounts specified in paragraph (c).</p>
          <p>(b) In the case of property located within the boundaries of a school district which qualifies as a tax relief area under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (b)</a>, but which is outside the boundaries of a municipality which meets the qualifications prescribed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (a)</a>, 57 percent of the tax, provided that the reduction shall not exceed the maximum amounts specified in paragraph (c).</p>
          <p>(c) The maximum reduction of the tax is $515 on property described in paragraph (a) and paragraph (b).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.135.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.</h2>
          <p>MS 1988 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.3.34.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 3 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.135.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Certification to commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>Not later than December 1 of each year, each county auditor having jurisdiction over one or more tax relief areas shall certify to the commissioner of revenue an estimate of the total amount of the reduction, determined under subdivision 2, in taxes payable the next succeeding year with respect to all tax relief areas in the county.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.135.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 1980 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/1/1/#laws.10.30.0">1Sp1981 c 1 art 10 s 30</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.135.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Reduction additional.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this section, the amount of property tax to be paid shall be determined after the allowance of any reduction prescribed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>, and the reduction prescribed by this section shall be in addition to that prescribed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.135.history
			">

      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.136">
        <h1 class="shn">273.136 TACONITE PROPERTY TAX RELIEF ACCOUNT; REPLACEMENT OF REVENUE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.136.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Payment from county.</span></h2>
          <p>Payment from the county shall be made as provided herein for the purpose of replacing revenue lost as a result of the reduction of property taxes provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.135">273.135</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.136.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Reduction amounts submitted to county.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of revenue shall determine, not later than April 1 of each year, the amount of reduction resulting from section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.135">273.135</a> in each county containing a tax relief area as defined by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134</a>, paragraph (b), basing determinations on a review of information reported to the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.85#stat.270C.85.2">270C.85, subdivision 2</a>, clause (4). The commissioner may make changes as deemed necessary. The commissioner of revenue, after such review, shall submit to the St. Louis County auditor, on or before April 15, the amount of the first half payment payable hereunder and on or before September 15 the amount of the second half payment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.136.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Payment times.</span></h2>
          <p>The St. Louis County auditor shall pay out of the taconite property tax relief account to each county treasurer one-half of the amount certified under subdivision 2 not later than May 15 and the remaining half not later than October 15 of each year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.136.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Distribution of funds.</span></h2>
          <p>The county treasurer shall distribute as part of the May and October settlements the funds received as if they had been collected as a part of the property tax reduced by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.135">273.135</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.136.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/1156/#laws.0.6.0">1969 c 1156 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/582/#laws.0.3.0">1973 c 582 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/775/#laws.0.3.0">1973 c 775 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/1/3/#laws.0.2.0">1Sp1981 c 3 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/14/#laws.10.3.0">1Sp1985 c 14 art 10 s 3</a>-6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/1/1/#laws.4.22.0">1Sp1986 c 1 art 4 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.6.7.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 6 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.18.10.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 18 s 10</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.137"><b>273.137</b> MS 1990 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/291/#laws.12.35.0">1991 c 291 art 12 s 35</a>]</div>

          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/21/#laws.6.17.0">1Sp2003 c 21 art 6 s 17</a>]</p>

        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.138.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/342/#laws.5.16.0">1983 c 342 art 5 s 16</a>]</p>

          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/21/#laws.6.17.0">1Sp2003 c 21 art 6 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.138.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/21/#laws.6.17.0">1Sp2003 c 21 art 6 s 17</a>]</p>

      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1381"><b>273.1381</b> MS 1994 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/587/#laws.3.21.0">1994 c 587 art 3 s 21</a> para (b)]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1382"><b>273.1382</b> MS 2000 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.96.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 96</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1383"><b>273.1383</b> MS 2012 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.9.94.0">2014 c 308 art 9 s 94</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1384">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1384 AGRICULTURAL HOMESTEAD MARKET VALUE CREDIT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1384.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/7/#laws.6.27.0">1Sp2011 c 7 art 6 s 27</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1384.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Agricultural homestead market value credit.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Property classified as agricultural homestead under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13#stat.273.13.23">273.13, subdivision 23</a>, paragraph (a), is eligible for an agricultural credit. The credit is computed using the property's agricultural credit market value, defined for this purpose as the property's market value excluding the market value of the house, garage, and immediately surrounding one acre of land. The credit is equal to 0.3 percent of the first $115,000 of the property's agricultural credit market value plus 0.1 percent of the property's agricultural credit market value in excess of $115,000, subject to a maximum credit of $490 for a full agricultural homestead. In the case of property that is classified as part homestead and part nonhomestead solely because not all the owners occupy or farm the property, not all the owners have qualifying relatives occupying or farming the property, or solely because not all the spouses of owners occupy the property, the credit is computed on the amount of agricultural credit market value corresponding to the percentage of homestead, and the maximum credit equals $490 multiplied by the percentage of homestead.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1384.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Credit reimbursements.</span></h2>
          <p>The county auditor shall determine the tax reductions allowed under subdivision 2 within the county for each taxes payable year and shall certify that amount to the commissioner of revenue under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.85#stat.270C.85.2">270C.85, subdivision 2</a>, clause (4). Any prior year adjustments shall also be certified. The commissioner shall review the certifications for accuracy, and may make such changes as are deemed necessary, or return the certification to the county auditor for correction. The credit under this section must be used to proportionately reduce the net tax capacity-based property tax payable to each local taxing jurisdiction as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1393">273.1393</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1384.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Payment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner of revenue shall reimburse each local taxing jurisdiction, other than school districts, for the tax reductions granted under subdivision 2 in two equal installments on October 31 and December 26 of the taxes payable year for which the reductions are granted, including in each payment the prior year adjustments certified on the abstracts for that taxes payable year. The reimbursements related to tax increments shall be issued in one installment each year on December 26.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner of revenue shall certify the total of the tax reductions granted under subdivision 2 for each taxes payable year within each school district to the commissioner of the Department of Education and the commissioner of education shall pay the reimbursement amounts to each school district as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1392">273.1392</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1384.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>An amount sufficient to make the payments required by this section to taxing jurisdictions other than school districts is annually appropriated from the general fund to the commissioner of revenue. An amount sufficient to make the payments required by this section for school districts is annually appropriated from the general fund to the commissioner of education.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1384.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>MS 2010 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/7/#laws.6.27.0">1Sp2011 c 7 art 6 s 27</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1384.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.37.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/377/#laws.4.18.0">2002 c 377 art 4 s 18</a>,19; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.4.2.0">2005 c 151 art 4 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/259/#laws.5.3.0">2006 c 259 art 5 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/366/#laws.6.29.0">2008 c 366 art 6 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/215/#laws.13.2.0">2010 c 215 art 13 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/1/1/#laws.13.1.0">1Sp2010 c 1 art 13 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/7/#laws.6.4.0">1Sp2011 c 7 art 6 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.1.2.0">2014 c 308 art 1 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.4.1.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 4 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.4.17.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 4 s 17</a>; art 18 s 11</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1385">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1385 AID FOR PUBLIC EMPLOYEES RETIREMENT ASSOCIATION EMPLOYER CONTRIBUTION RATE INCREASE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1385.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Aid to offset rate increase.</span></h2>
          <p>Beginning with the December 26, 1997, payment, and according to the schedule for payment of local aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/477A.015">477A.015</a> thereafter, the commissioner of revenue shall pay to each city, county, town, and other nonschool jurisdiction an amount equal to 0.35 percent of the fiscal year 1997 payroll for employees who were members of the general plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association. Except for the December 1997 distribution under this section, the amount of aid must be certified before September 1 of the year preceding the distribution year to the affected local government. The executive director of the Public Employees Retirement Association shall certify the general plan fiscal year covered payroll and other information requested by the commissioner of revenue, on or before August 1, 1997, and in subsequent years where necessary, in order to facilitate administration of this section. The amount necessary to make these aid payments is appropriated annually from the general fund to the commissioner of revenue. Expenditures under this section are estimated to be $7,942,500 in fiscal year 1998, and $15,885,000 in each subsequent fiscal year, less any future reductions under subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1385.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Limit on aid and potential future permanent aid reductions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The aid amount received by any jurisdiction in fiscal year 2000 or any year thereafter may not exceed the amount it received in fiscal year 1999. The commissioner may, from time to time, request the most recent fiscal year payroll information by jurisdiction to be certified by the executive director of the Public Employees Retirement Association. For any jurisdiction where newly certified public employees retirement association general plan payroll is significantly lower than the fiscal 1997 amount, as determined by the commissioner, the commissioner shall recalculate the aid amount based on the most recent fiscal year payroll information, certify the recalculated aid amount for the next distribution year, and permanently reduce the aid amount to that jurisdiction.</p>
          <p>(b) Aid to a jurisdiction must not be reduced under this section due to a transfer of an employee from the general plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association to the local government correctional service plan administered by the Public Employees Retirement Association. The executive director of the Public Employees Retirement Association must provide the commissioner of revenue with any information requested by the commissioner to administer this paragraph.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1385.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Effect of reorganizations.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of revenue may adjust the aid amounts for separate jurisdictions to account for significant changes in boundaries or in the form of government, as determined by the commissioner. If a local government function and the associated Public Employees Retirement Association general plan payroll is assumed by either the state, or a nonpublic organization, the aid amounts attributable to the function under this section must terminate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1385.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Aid termination.</span></h2>
          <p>The aid provided under this section terminates on June 30, 2020.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1385.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/233/#laws.1.15.0">1997 c 233 art 1 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.2.3.0">1999 c 222 art 2 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1386"><b>273.1386</b> MS 2012 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.9.94.0">2014 c 308 art 9 s 94</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1387">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1387 SCHOOL BUILDING BOND AGRICULTURAL CREDIT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1387.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>All class 2a, 2b, and 2c property under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>, subdivision 23, other than property consisting of the house, garage, and immediately surrounding one acre of land of an agricultural homestead, is eligible to receive the credit under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1387.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Credit amount.</span></h2>
          <p>For each qualifying property, the school building bond agricultural credit is equal to 70 percent of the property's eligible net tax capacity multiplied by the school debt tax rate determined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/275.08#stat.275.08.1b">275.08, subdivision 1b</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1387.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Credit reimbursements.</span></h2>
          <p>The county auditor shall determine the tax reductions allowed under this section within the county for each taxes payable year and shall certify that amount to the commissioner of revenue under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.85#stat.270C.85.2">270C.85, subdivision 2</a>, clause (4). Any prior year adjustments shall also be certified. The commissioner shall review the certifications for accuracy, and may make such changes as are deemed necessary, or return the certification to the county auditor for correction. The credit under this section must be used to reduce the school district net tax capacity-based property tax as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1393">273.1393</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1387.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Payment.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner of revenue shall certify the total of the tax reductions granted under this section for each taxes payable year within each school district to the commissioner of education, who shall pay the reimbursement amounts to each school district as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1392">273.1392</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1387.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>An amount sufficient to make the payments required by this section is annually appropriated from the general fund to the commissioner of education.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1387.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.4.2.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 4 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.5.2.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 5 s 2</a>; art 18 s 12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/25/#laws.0.153.0">2023 c 25 s 153</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.139"><b>273.139</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/342/#laws.5.16.0">1983 c 342 art 5 s 16</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1391">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1391 SUPPLEMENTARY HOMESTEAD PROPERTY TAX RELIEF.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1391.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Homestead property tax reduction; tax relief area.</span></h2>
          <p>The property tax to be paid in respect to property taxable within a tax relief area described in subdivision 2 on homestead property, as otherwise determined by law and regardless of the market value of the property, for all purposes shall be reduced in the amount prescribed by subdivision 2, subject to the limitations contained therein.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1391.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Reduction amount.</span></h2>
          <p>The amount of the reduction authorized by subdivision 1 shall be:</p>
          <p>(a) In the case of property located within a school district which does not meet the qualifications of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (b)</a>, as a tax relief area, but which is located in a county with a population of less than 100,000 in which taconite is mined or quarried and wherein a school district is located which does meet the qualifications of a tax relief area, and provided that at least 90 percent of the area of the school district which does not meet the qualifications of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (b)</a>, lies within such county, 57 percent of the tax on qualified property located in the school district that does not meet the qualifications of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (b)</a>, provided that the amount of said reduction shall not exceed the maximum amounts specified in paragraph (d). The reduction provided by this paragraph shall only be applicable to property located within the boundaries of the county described therein.</p>
          <p>(b) In the case of property located within a school district which does not meet the qualifications of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (b)</a>, as a tax relief area, but which is located in a school district in a county containing a city of the first class and a municipality as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (a)</a>, but not in a school district containing a city of the first class or adjacent to a school district containing a city of the first class unless the school district so adjacent contains a municipality as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (a)</a>, 57 percent of the tax, but not to exceed the maximums specified in paragraph (d).</p>
          <p>(c) In the case of property located within the boundaries of a municipality that meets the qualifications in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (a)</a>, but not the qualifications of a tax relief area in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (b)</a>, 66 percent of the tax, provided that the reduction shall not exceed $315.10. In the case of property located within the boundaries of a school district which qualifies as a taconite assistance area under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1341">273.1341</a>, but does not qualify as a tax relief area under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.134">273.134, paragraph (b)</a>, but which is outside the boundaries of a municipality which meets the qualifications of the preceding sentence, 57 percent of the tax, provided that the reduction shall not exceed the maximum amounts specified in paragraph (d).</p>
          <p>(d) Except as otherwise provided in this section, the maximum reduction of the tax is $289.80.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1391.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.</h2>
          <p>MS 1988 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.3.34.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 3 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1391.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Certification; payment by commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>Not later than December 1, each county auditor having jurisdiction over one or more tax relief areas defined in subdivision 2 shall certify to the commissioner of revenue an estimate of the total amount of the reduction, determined under subdivision 2, in taxes payable the next succeeding year with respect to all tax relief areas in the auditor's county. The commissioner shall make payments to the county at the times provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/477A.015">477A.015</a>. The county treasurer shall distribute as part of the May and October settlements the funds received from the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1391.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Additional reduction.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this section, the amount of property tax to be paid shall be determined after the allowance of any reduction prescribed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>, and the reduction prescribed by this section shall be in addition to that prescribed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1391.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>A sum sufficient to make the payments required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/477A.15">477A.15</a> and this section is annually appropriated from the general fund to the commissioner of revenue for the purpose of funding those sections.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1391.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/607/#laws.7.7.0">1980 c 607 art 7 s 7</a>,11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/342/#laws.2.22.0">1983 c 342 art 2 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/502/#laws.7.5.0">1984 c 502 art 7 s 5</a>,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/593/#laws.0.31.0">1984 c 593 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/14/#laws.4.64.0">1Sp1985 c 14 art 4 s 64</a>,65; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/1/1/#laws.4.23.0">1Sp1986 c 1 art 4 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.6.26.0">1987 c 268 art 6 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.23.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 23</a>,82,83; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/277/#laws.2.31.0">1989 c 277 art 2 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.2.11.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 2 s 11</a>; art 3 s 12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/480/#laws.7.8.0">1990 c 480 art 7 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/511/#laws.4.7.0">1992 c 511 art 4 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/389/#laws.10.3.0">1998 c 389 art 10 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.6.8.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 6 s 8</a>,9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.11.5.0">2003 c 127 art 11 s 5</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1392">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1392 PAYMENT; SCHOOL DISTRICTS.</h1>
        <p>The amounts of bovine tuberculosis credit reimbursements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.113">273.113</a>; conservation tax credits under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.119">273.119</a>; disaster or emergency reimbursement under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1231">273.1231</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1235">273.1235</a>; agricultural credits under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1384">273.1384</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1387">273.1387</a>; aids and credits under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1398">273.1398</a>; enterprise zone property credit payments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.171">469.171</a>; metropolitan agricultural preserve reduction under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473H.10">473H.10</a>; and electric generation transition aid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/477A.24">477A.24</a> for school districts, shall be certified to the Department of Education by the Department of Revenue. The amounts so certified shall be paid according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/127A.45">127A.45, subdivisions 9</a>, 10, and 13.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1392.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/641/#laws.2.12.0">1982 c 641 art 2 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/342/#laws.7.5.0">1983 c 342 art 7 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/14/#laws.4.66.0">1Sp1985 c 14 art 4 s 66</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.5.6.0">1987 c 268 art 5 s 6</a>; art 6 s 27; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.24.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.3.13.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 3 s 13</a>; art 9 s 25; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/199/#laws.2.19.0">1991 c 199 art 2 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/1/1/#laws.2.4.0">1Sp1993 c 1 art 2 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/1/3/#laws.16.13.0">1Sp1995 c 3 art 16 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/31/#laws.3.6.0">1997 c 31 art 3 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/397/#laws.11.3.0">1998 c 397 art 11 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.38.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/377/#laws.10.7.0">2002 c 377 art 10 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/130/#laws.0.12.0">2003 c 130 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/228/#laws.3.5.0">2004 c 228 art 3 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/1/2/#laws.3.11.0">1Sp2007 c 2 art 3 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/389/#laws.8.9.0">2010 c 389 art 8 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.1.46.0">2012 c 187 art 1 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/40/#laws.1.93.0">2017 c 40 art 1 s 93</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.4.3.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 4 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/64/#laws.4.1.0">2023 c 64 art 4 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1393">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1393 COMPUTATION OF NET PROPERTY TAXES.</h1>
        <p class="in">Notwithstanding any other provisions to the contrary, "net" property taxes are determined by subtracting the credits in the order listed from the gross tax:</p>
        <p class="in">(1) disaster credit as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1231">273.1231</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1235">273.1235</a>;</p>
        <p class="in">(2) powerline credit as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.42">273.42</a>;</p>
        <p class="in">(3) agricultural preserves credit as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473H.10">473H.10</a>;</p>
        <p class="in">(4) enterprise zone credit as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.171">469.171</a>;</p>
        <p class="in">(5) disparity reduction credit;</p>
        <p class="in">(6) conservation tax credit as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.119">273.119</a>;</p>
        <p class="in">(7) the school bond credit as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1387">273.1387</a>;</p>
        <p class="in">(8) agricultural credit as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1384">273.1384</a>;</p>
        <p class="in">(9) taconite homestead credit as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.135">273.135</a>;</p>
        <p class="in">(10) supplemental homestead credit as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1391">273.1391</a>; and</p>
        <p class="in">(11) the bovine tuberculosis zone credit, as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.113">273.113</a>.</p>
        <p class="in">The combination of all property tax credits must not exceed the gross tax amount.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1393.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/300/#laws.0.8.0">1985 c 300 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/14/#laws.4.67.0">1Sp1985 c 14 art 4 s 67</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.5.7.0">1987 c 268 art 5 s 7</a>; art 6 s 28; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.210.0">1987 c 291 s 210</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.25.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/277/#laws.2.32.0">1989 c 277 art 2 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/231/#laws.1.13.0">1997 c 231 art 1 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.39.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/1/2/#laws.3.11.0">1Sp2007 c 2 art 3 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/366/#laws.6.30.0">2008 c 366 art 6 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/7/#laws.6.6.0">1Sp2011 c 7 art 6 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.4.4.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 4 s 4</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1394"><b>273.1394</b> MS 1987 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.81.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 81</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1395"><b>273.1395</b> MS 1987 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.81.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 81</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1396"><b>273.1396</b> MS 1987 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.81.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 81</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1397"><b>273.1397</b> MS 1987 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.81.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 81</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1398">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1398 DISPARITY REDUCTION AID AND CREDIT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) In this section, the terms defined in this subdivision have the meanings given them.</p>
          <p>(b) "Unique taxing jurisdiction" means the geographic area subject to the same set of local tax rates.</p>
          <p>(c) For purposes of calculating and allocating disparity reduction aid authorized in subdivision 3, "gross taxes levied on all properties," "gross taxes," or "taxes levied" means the total net tax capacity based taxes levied on all properties except that levied on the captured value of tax increment districts as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.177#stat.469.177.2">469.177, subdivision 2</a>, and that levied on the portion of commercial industrial properties' assessed value or gross tax capacity, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473F.02#stat.473F.02.3">473F.02, subdivision 3</a>, subject to the areawide tax as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473F.08#stat.473F.08.6">473F.08, subdivision 6</a>, in a unique taxing jurisdiction. "Gross taxes" are before any reduction for disparity reduction aid but "taxes levied" are after any reduction for disparity reduction aid. Gross taxes levied or taxes levied cannot be less than zero.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/228/#laws.3.18.0">2004 c 228 art 3 s 18</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/21/#laws.6.17.0">1Sp2003 c 21 art 6 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.2a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.</h2>
          <p>MS 1993 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/416/#laws.1.65.0">1994 c 416 art 1 s 65</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.2b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.</h2>
          <p>MS 1990 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/604/#laws.4.21.0">1990 c 604 art 4 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.2c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2c.</h2>
          <p>MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/21/#laws.6.17.0">1Sp2003 c 21 art 6 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.2d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2d.<span class="headnote">Aids determined as of June 30.</span></h2>
          <p>For aid amounts authorized under subdivision 3: (i) if the effective date for a municipal incorporation, consolidation, annexation, detachment, dissolution, or township organization is on or before June 30 of the year preceding the aid distribution year, the change in boundaries or form of government shall be recognized for aid determinations for the aid distribution year; (ii) if the effective date for a municipal incorporation, consolidation, annexation, detachment, dissolution, or township organization is after June 30 of the year preceding the aid distribution year, the change in boundaries or form of government shall not be recognized for aid determinations until the following year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.2e">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2e.</h2>
          <p>MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/228/#laws.3.18.0">2004 c 228 art 3 s 18</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Disparity reduction aid.</span></h2>
          <p class="in"> The amount of disparity aid certified for each taxing district within each unique taxing jurisdiction is the amount certified for taxes payable in the prior year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Disparity reduction aid to cities.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding the provisions of subdivision 3 or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/275.08#stat.275.08.1d">275.08, subdivision 1d</a>, the amount of disparity reduction aid for a city for aid payable in calendar year 1994 and thereafter is zero, and the local tax rate for taxes payable in 1994 and thereafter for a city shall not be adjusted under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/275.08#stat.275.08.1d">275.08, subdivision 1d</a>. For purposes of this subdivision, city means a statutory or home rule charter city.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Disparity reduction credit.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Class 4a and class 3a property qualifies for a disparity reduction credit if the property is located in a border city that is eligible to have an enterprise zone, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/469.166">469.166</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The credit is an amount sufficient to reduce (i) the taxes levied on class 4a property to 1.6 percent of the property's taxable market value and (ii) the tax on class 3a property to 1.6 percent of taxable market value.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The county auditor shall annually certify the costs of the credits to the Department of Revenue. The department shall reimburse local governments for the property taxes forgone as the result of the credits in proportion to their total levies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2006 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/13/#laws.2.11.0">2007 c 13 art 2 s 11</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.4b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.</h2>
          <p>MS 2012 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.9.94.0">2014 c 308 art 9 s 94</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.4c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4c.</h2>
          <p>MS 2006 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/13/#laws.2.11.0">2007 c 13 art 2 s 11</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.4d">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4d.</h2>
          <p>MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/21/#laws.6.17.0">1Sp2003 c 21 art 6 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 1992 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/375/#laws.4.21.0">1993 c 375 art 4 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.5a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5a.</h2>
          <p>MS 1992 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/1/1/#laws.2.7.0">1Sp1993 c 1 art 2 s 7</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.5b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5b.</h2>
          <p>MS 1994 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/471/#laws.3.55.0">1996 c 471 art 3 s 55</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.5c">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5c.</h2>
          <p>MS 1992 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/1/1/#laws.2.7.0">1Sp1993 c 1 art 2 s 7</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Payment.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall certify the aids provided in subdivision 3 before September 1 of the year preceding the distribution year to the county auditor of the affected local government. The aids provided in subdivision 3, must be paid to local governments other than school districts at the times provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/477A.015">477A.015</a> for payment of local government aid to taxing jurisdictions, except that the first one-half payment of disparity reduction aid provided in subdivision 3 must be paid on or before August 31. The disparity reduction credit provided in subdivision 4 must be paid to taxing jurisdictions other than school districts at the time provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473H.10#stat.473H.10.3">473H.10, subdivision 3</a>. Aids and credit reimbursements to school districts must be certified to the commissioner of education and paid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.1392">273.1392</a>. Payment shall not be made to any taxing jurisdiction that has ceased to levy a property tax.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>MS 1995 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/587/#laws.3.21.0">1994 c 587 art 3 s 21</a> para (b)]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1398.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Appropriation.</span></h2>
          <p>An amount sufficient to pay the aids and credits provided under this section for school districts, intermediate school districts, or any group of school districts levying as a single taxing entity, is annually appropriated from the general fund to the commissioner of education. An amount sufficient to pay the aids and credits provided under this section for counties, cities, towns, and special taxing districts is annually appropriated from the general fund to the commissioner of revenue. A jurisdiction's aid amount may be increased or decreased based on any prior year adjustments for property tax credit or aid programs.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1398.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.26.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 26</a>,84; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/277/#laws.2.33.0">1989 c 277 art 2 s 33</a>-36; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.6.47.0">1989 c 329 art 6 s 47</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.0.0.0">1Sp1989 c 1</a>; art 2 s 11; art 3 s 14-21,33; art 6 s 8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/480/#laws.7.9.0">1990 c 480 art 7 s 9</a>-13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/604/#laws.3.20.0">1990 c 604 art 3 s 20</a>; art 4 s 2-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/130/#laws.0.37.0">1991 c 130 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/199/#laws.1.62.0">1991 c 199 art 1 s 62</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/291/#laws.3.1.0">1991 c 291 art 3 s 1</a>-4; art 12 s 10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/292/#laws.7.22.0">1991 c 292 art 7 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/499/#laws.12.29.0">1992 c 499 art 12 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/511/#laws.1.8.0">1992 c 511 art 1 s 8</a>,9; art 3 s 1; art 4 s 8,27; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/224/#laws.1.28.0">1993 c 224 art 1 s 28</a>,29; art 14 s 16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/375/#laws.3.18.0">1993 c 375 art 3 s 18</a>-20; art 4 s 3-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/1/1/#laws.2.5.0">1Sp1993 c 1 art 2 s 5</a>;       </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.1399"><b>273.1399</b> MS 2000 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.96.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 96</a>; art 15 s 41]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.14">
        <h1 class="shn">273.14 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.14.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Words, terms, and phrases.</span></h2>
          <p>Unless the language or context clearly indicates that a different meaning is intended, the following words, terms, and phrases, for the purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.14">273.14</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.16">273.16</a>, shall be given the meanings subjoined to them.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.14.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Person.</span></h2>
          <p>The word "person" shall be construed to include individuals, copartnerships, companies, joint stock companies, corporations, and all associations, however and for whatever purpose organized.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.14.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Deposit.</span></h2>
          <p>The word "deposit" means a body of iron-bearing materials which, in accordance with good engineering and metallurgical practice, should be mined as a unit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.14.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Low-grade iron-bearing formations.</span></h2>
          <p>"Low-grade iron-bearing formations" mean those commercial deposits of iron-bearing materials, not including paint rock, located beneath the surface of the earth, which in their natural state require beneficiation to make them suitable for blast furnace use, and which, after such beneficiation, produce in tonnage less than 50 percent of iron ore concentrates from the tonnage of low-grade iron-bearing formations delivered to a beneficiation plant and which formations must be mined in accordance with good engineering and metallurgical practice to produce such concentrates.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.14.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Beneficiation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Beneficiation" means the process of concentrating that portion of the iron-bearing formations entering the beneficiating plant.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.14.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Concentrates.</span></h2>
          <p>"Concentrates" means such ores which by the process of beneficiation have been made suitable for blast furnace use.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.14.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Tonnage recovery or tonnage recovery of iron ore concentrates.</span></h2>
          <p>The term "tonnage recovery" or "tonnage recovery of iron ore concentrates" means the proportion which the weight of concentrates recovered or recoverable after beneficiation bears to the weight of the low-grade iron-bearing materials entering the beneficiating plant.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.14.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">1993-2</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1937/0/364/#laws.0.1.0">1937 c 364 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.15"><b>273.15</b> MS 1984 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/14/#laws.4.98.0">1Sp1985 c 14 art 4 s 98</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.16">
        <h1 class="shn">273.16 DETERMINATION OF CLASSIFICATION.</h1>
        <p>The classification of iron-bearing formations under the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.14">273.14</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.16">273.16</a> shall be determined in the manner provided. Any person engaged in the business of mining, whose tonnage recovery of iron ore concentrates for a taxable year in producing concentrates from the iron-bearing material entering the beneficiating plant has been less than 50 percent, may file a petition with the commissioner of revenue requesting classification of the deposit under the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.14">273.14</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.16">273.16</a>. The taxpayer shall furnish any available data and information concerning the operation of the deposit as the commissioner of revenue requires.
