The commissioner shall, upon receipt of it, submit the petition and data to the University of Minnesota mines experiment station. The mines experiment station shall consider the deposit referred to in the petition as a unified commercial operation. Based on all engineering data and information furnished, it shall file a written report with the commissioner of revenue, who, after hearing, shall approve or disapprove the report. If a classification is made covering the deposit and property, the commissioner of revenue shall give appropriate notice of it to the taxing districts affected by it. If the commissioner of revenue disapproves of the classification, the commissioner's findings and order on it may be reviewed by the court of appeals on petition of the party aggrieved presented to the court within 30 days after the date of the order. The classifications shall also be subject to further review by the mines experiment station, from time to time, upon request of the commissioner of revenue or upon further petition by the taxpayer. Valuations determined hereunder shall be subject to the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.921">270C.921</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.928">270C.928</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.16.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">1993-4</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1937/0/364/#laws.0.3.0">1937 c 364 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/582/#laws.0.3.0">1973 c 582 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/247/#laws.0.119.0">1983 c 247 s 119</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.2.17.0">2005 c 151 art 2 s 17</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.165">
        <h1 class="shn">273.165 TAXATION OF SEPARATE MINERAL INTERESTS AND UNMINED IRON ORE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.165.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Mineral interest.</span></h2>
          <p>"Mineral interest," for the purpose of this subdivision, means an interest in any minerals, including but not limited to gas, coal, oil, or other similar interest in real estate, which is owned separately and apart from the fee title to the surface of such real property. Mineral interests which are recorded in the office of either the county recorder or registrar of titles, whether or not filed pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/93.52">93.52</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/93.551">93.551</a>, are taxed as provided in this subdivision unless specifically excluded by this subdivision. A tax of 40 cents per acre or portion of an acre of mineral interest is imposed and is payable annually. If an interest is a fractional undivided interest in an area, the tax due on the interest per acre or portion of an acre is equal to the product obtained by multiplying the fractional interest times 40 cents, computed to the nearest cent. However, the minimum annual tax on any mineral interest is $3.20. No such tax on mineral interests is imposed on the following: (1) mineral interests valued and taxed under other laws relating to the taxation of minerals, gas, coal, oil, or other similar interests; or (2) mineral interests which are exempt from taxation pursuant to constitutional or related statutory provisions. Taxes received under this subdivision must be apportioned to the taxing districts included in the area taxed in the same proportion as the surface interest local tax rate of a taxing district bears to the total local tax rate applicable to surface interests in the area taxed. The tax imposed by this subdivision is not included within any limitations as to rate or amount of taxes which may be imposed in an area to which the tax imposed by this subdivision applies. The tax imposed by this subdivision does not cause the amount of other taxes levied or to be levied in the area, which are subject to any such limitation, to be reduced in any amount. Twenty percent of the revenues received from the tax imposed by this subdivision must be distributed under the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/116J.64">116J.64</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.165.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Iron ore.</span></h2>
          <p>Unmined iron ore included in class 5, paragraph (b), must be assessed with and as a part of the real estate in which it is located, but its net tax capacity would be as established in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13#stat.273.13.31">273.13, subdivision 31</a>. The real estate in which iron ore is located, other than the ore, must be classified and assessed in accordance with the provisions of the appropriate classes. In assessing any tract or lot of real estate in which iron ore is known to exist, the assessable net tax capacity of the ore exclusive of the land in which it is located, and the assessable net tax capacity of the land exclusive of the ore must be determined and set down separately and the aggregate of the two must be assessed against the tract or lot.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.165.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/14/#laws.4.68.0">1Sp1985 c 14 art 4 s 68</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.6.32.0">1987 c 268 art 6 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.27.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 27</a>,84; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.13.20.0">1989 c 329 art 13 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.2.11.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 2 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/587/#laws.5.12.0">1994 c 587 art 5 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/4/#laws.0.34.0">2005 c 4 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/25/#laws.0.154.0">2023 c 25 s 154</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.1651">
        <h1 class="shn">273.1651 TAXATION AND FORFEITURE OF STOCKPILED METALLIC MINERALS MATERIAL.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1651.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definition.</span></h2>
          <p>"Stockpiled metallic minerals material," for purposes of this section, means surface overburden, rock, lean ore, tailings, or other material that has been removed from the ground and deposited elsewhere on the surface in the process of iron ore, taconite, or other metallic minerals mining, or in the process of beneficiation. Stockpiled metallic minerals material does not include processed metallic minerals concentrates in the form of pellets, chips, briquettes, fines, or other form which have been prepared for or are in the process of shipment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1651.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Purpose.</span></h2>
          <p>The purpose of this section is to clarify the ownership of stockpiled metallic minerals material in this state. Depending on the intent of the person who extracted the material from the ground, stockpiled metallic minerals material may or may not be owned separately and apart from the fee title to the surface of the real property. The legislature finds that the uncertainty of ownership of stockpiled metallic minerals material located on real property that becomes tax forfeited has created a burden on the public owner of the surface of the real property and an impediment to productive management or use of a public resource.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1651.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Taxation and forfeiture.</span></h2>
          <p>From and after July 1, 1997, for purposes of taxation, the definition of "real property," as contained in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/272.03#stat.272.03.1">272.03, subdivision 1</a>, includes stockpiled metallic minerals material. Nothing in this subdivision shall be construed to subject stockpiled metallic minerals material to the general property tax when the stockpiled metallic minerals material is exempt from the general property tax pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/298.015">298.015</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/298.25">298.25</a>. If the surface of the real property forfeits for delinquent taxes, stockpiled metallic minerals material located on the real property forfeits with the surface of the property.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1651.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Prior forfeiture.</span></h2>
          <p>Stockpiled metallic minerals material located on real property that forfeited prior to July 1, 1997, or forfeits due to a judgment for delinquent taxes issued prior to July 1, 1997, shall be assessed and taxed as real property. The tax applies only to stockpiled metallic minerals material located on real property that remains in the ownership of the state or a political subdivision of the state. The tax shall be based on the market value of the rental of the property for storage of stockpiled metallic minerals material.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1651.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Exceptions; tax laws.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The tax imposed pursuant to this section shall not be imposed on the following:</p>
          <p>(1) stockpiled metallic minerals material valued and taxed under other laws relating to the taxation of minerals, gas, coal, oil, or other similar interests;</p>
          <p>(2) stockpiled metallic minerals material that is exempt from taxation pursuant to constitutional or related statutory provisions; or</p>
          <p>(3) stockpiled metallic minerals material that is owned by the state.</p>
          <p>(b) All laws for the enforcement of taxes on real property shall apply to the tax imposed pursuant to this section on stockpiled metallic minerals material.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.1651.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Fee owner.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/276.041">276.041</a>, the owner of stockpiled metallic minerals material is a fee owner.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.1651.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/231/#laws.8.4.0">1997 c 231 art 8 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.166"><b>273.166</b> MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/21/#laws.6.17.0">1Sp2003 c 21 art 6 s 17</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.17">
        <h1 class="shn">273.17 ASSESSMENT OF REAL PROPERTY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.17.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Property additions; classification changes.</span></h2>
          <p>In every year, on January 2, the assessor shall also assess all real property that may have become subject to taxation since the last previous assessment, including all real property platted since the last real estate assessment, and all buildings or other structures of any kind, whether completed or in process of construction, of over $1,000 in value, the value of which has not been previously added to or included in the valuation of the land on which they have been erected. The assessor shall make return thereof to the county auditor, with a return of personal property, showing the tract or lot on which each structure has been erected and the market value added thereto by such erection. Every assessor shall list, without revaluing, in each year, on a form to be prescribed by the commissioner of revenue, all parcels of land that shall have become homesteads or shall have ceased to be homesteads for taxation purposes since the last real estate assessment, and other parcels of land when the use of the land requires a change in classification or the land has been incorrectly classified in a previous assessment.</p>
          <p>The county auditor shall note such change in the net tax capacity upon the tax lists, caused by a change in classification, and shall calculate the taxes for such year on such changed net tax capacity. In case of the destruction by fire, flood, or otherwise of any building or structure, over $100 in value, which has been erected previous to the last valuation of the land on which it stood, or the value of which has been added to any former valuation, the assessor shall determine, as nearly as practicable, how much less such land would sell for at private sale in consequence of such destruction, and make return thereof to the auditor.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.17.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Record of changes.</span></h2>
          <p>In counties where the county auditor has elected to discontinue the preparation of assessment books as provided by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.03#stat.273.03.2">273.03, subdivision 2</a>, such changes as provided for in subdivision 1, shall be recorded in a separate record prepared under the direction of the county assessor and shall identify, by description or property identification number, or both, the real estate affected, the previous year's net tax capacities and the new market values and net tax capacities, provided that if only property identification numbers are used they shall be such that shall permit positive identification of the real estate to which they apply. Such record shall further indicate the total amount of increase or decrease in net tax capacity contained therein. The county assessor shall make return of such record to the county auditor who shall be the official custodian thereof.</p>
          <p>Such record shall be known as "County assessor's changes in real estate valuations for the year." Such records on file in the county auditor's office may be destroyed when they are more than ten years old pursuant to the conditions for destruction of government records contained in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/138.161">138.161</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/138.25">138.25</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.17.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">1994</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 811</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1917/0/254/#laws.0.0.0">1917 c 254</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1937/0/206/#laws.0.1.0">1937 c 206 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/781/#laws.0.2.0">1963 c 781 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/578/#laws.0.2.0">1967 c 578 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/582/#laws.0.3.0">1973 c 582 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/376/#laws.0.1.0">1974 c 376 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/339/#laws.0.4.0">1975 c 339 s 4</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/437/#laws.8.8.0">1975 c 437 art 8 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/345/#laws.0.2.0">1976 c 345 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/303/#laws.2.19.0">1979 c 303 art 2 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.84.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 84</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.13.20.0">1989 c 329 art 13 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/264/#laws.16.11.0">1995 c 264 art 16 s 11</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.18">
        <h1 class="shn">273.18 LISTING, VALUATION, AND ASSESSMENT OF EXEMPT PROPERTY BY COUNTY AUDITORS.</h1>
        <p>(a) In every sixth year after the year 2010, the county auditor shall enter the description of each tract of real property exempt by law from taxation, with the name of the owner, and the assessor shall value and assess the same in the same manner that other real property is valued and assessed, and shall designate in each case the purpose for which the property is used.</p>
        <p>(b) The county auditor shall include in the exempt property information that the commissioner may require under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.85#stat.270C.85.2">270C.85, subdivision 2</a>, clause (4), the total number of acres of all natural resources lands for which in lieu payments are made under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/477A.11">477A.11</a> to 477A.14 and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/477A.17">477A.17</a>. The assessor shall estimate its market value, provided that if the assessor is not able to estimate the market value of the land on a per parcel basis, the assessor shall furnish the commissioner of revenue with an estimate of the average value per acre of this land within the county.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.18.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">1995</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 812</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1925/0/211/#laws.0.1.0">1925 c 211 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/231/#laws.2.22.0">1997 c 231 art 2 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.9.31.0">2014 c 308 art 9 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.18.13.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 18 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/1/14/#laws.13.9.0">1Sp2021 c 14 art 13 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.19">
        <h1 class="shn">273.19 LESSEES AND EQUITABLE OWNERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.19.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Tax-exempt property; lease.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Except as provided in subdivision 3 or 4, tax-exempt property held under a lease for a term of at least one year, and not taxable under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/272.01#stat.272.01.2">272.01, subdivision 2</a>, or under a contract for the purchase thereof, shall be considered, for all purposes of taxation, as the property of the person holding it. In this subdivision, "tax-exempt property" means property owned by the United States, the state or any of its political subdivisions, a school, or any religious, scientific, or benevolent society or institution, incorporated or unincorporated, or any corporation whose property is not taxed in the same manner as other property. This subdivision does not apply to property exempt from taxation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/272.01#stat.272.01.2">272.01, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (b), clauses (2), (3), and (4), or to property exempt from taxation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/272.0213">272.0213</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.19.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Lease defined.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section, a lease includes any agreement, except a cooperative farming agreement pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/97A.135#stat.97A.135.3">97A.135, subdivision 3</a>, or a lease executed pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/272.68#stat.272.68.4">272.68, subdivision 4</a>, permitting a nonexempt person or entity to use the property, regardless of whether the agreement is characterized as a lease. A lease has a "term of at least one year" if the term is for a period of less than one year and the lease permits the parties to renew the lease without requiring that similar terms for leasing the property will be offered to other applicants or bidders through a competitive bidding or other form of offer to potential lessees or users.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.19.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Seaway port authority property; exception.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of subdivision 1 shall not apply to any property owned by a seaway port authority exempt from taxation under the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/272.01#stat.272.01.3">272.01, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.19.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Property located within a federal reservation.</span></h2>
          <p>The net tax capacity of property held under a lease for a term of at least one year which (i) is located within a federal reservation; (ii) has been conveyed to the state of Minnesota by the federal government; and (iii) had been occupied and used by a branch of the armed services of the United States, shall be no greater than the value added to the property by improvements to the property made by the lessee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.19.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Property located within a national park.</span></h2>
          <p>Property held under a lease for a term of at least one year which is owned by the United States and located within a national park shall be exempt, provided the property was acquired by the United States by condemnation or purchased by the United States under threat of condemnation, and within a reasonable time leased back for noncommercial residential purposes to the person owning the property at the time of acquisition by the United States. If property exempt under this subdivision is subsequently leased or subleased for a term of at least one year to another person, it shall no longer qualify for the exemption provided in this subdivision and shall be placed on the assessment rolls as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/272.02#stat.272.02.38">272.02, subdivision 38</a>, and taxed pursuant to subdivision 1 of this section.</p>
          <p>The value of improvements made to property otherwise exempt pursuant to this subdivision which are owned by the lessee or to which the lessee has salvage rights shall be taxable to the lessee pursuant to subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.19.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 2004 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.5.46.0">2005 c 151 art 5 s 46</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.19.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">1996</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 813</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/1/#laws.0.2.0">Ex1959 c 1 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/865/#laws.0.2.0">1967 c 865 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/756/#laws.0.1.0">1978 c 756 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/607/#laws.2.16.0">1980 c 607 art 2 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/1/1/#laws.2.13.0">1Sp1981 c 1 art 2 s 13</a>,14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/502/#laws.3.15.0">1984 c 502 art 3 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/300/#laws.0.9.0">1985 c 300 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.8.4.0">1987 c 268 art 8 s 4</a>-7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.84.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 84</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/239/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 239 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.13.20.0">1989 c 329 art 13 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/391/#laws.8.34.0">1990 c 391 art 8 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.5.23.0">2005 c 151 art 5 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/366/#laws.6.31.0">2008 c 366 art 6 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/143/#laws.17.10.0">2013 c 143 art 17 s 10</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.20">
        <h1 class="shn">273.20 ASSESSOR MAY ENTER DWELLINGS, BUILDINGS, OR STRUCTURES.</h1>
        <p>Any officer authorized by law to assess property for taxation may, when necessary to the proper performance of duties, enter any dwelling-house, building, or structure, and view the same and the property therein.</p>
        <p>Any officer authorized by law to assess property for ad valorem tax purposes shall have reasonable access to land and structures as necessary for the proper performance of their duties. A property owner may refuse to allow an assessor to inspect their property. This refusal by the property owner must be either verbal or expressly stated in a letter to the county assessor. If the assessor is denied access to view a property, the assessor is authorized to estimate the property's estimated market value by making assumptions believed appropriate concerning the property's finish and condition.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.20.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">1997</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 814</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/243/#laws.5.24.0">1999 c 243 art 5 s 24</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.21">
        <h1 class="shn">273.21 NEGLECT BY AUDITOR OR ASSESSOR; PENALTY.</h1>
        <p>Every county auditor and every town or district assessor who in any case refuses or knowingly neglects to perform any duty enjoined by this chapter, or who consents to or connives at any evasion of its provisions whereby any proceeding required by this chapter is prevented or hindered, or whereby any property required to be listed for taxation is unlawfully exempted, or entered on the tax list at less than its market value, shall, for every such neglect, refusal, consent, or connivance, forfeit and pay to the state not less than $200, nor more than $1,000, to be recovered in any court of competent jurisdiction.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.21.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">1998</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 815</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/339/#laws.0.8.0">1975 c 339 s 8</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.22"><b>273.22</b> MS 1992 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/416/#laws.1.65.0">1994 c 416 art 1 s 65</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.23"><b>273.23</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/222/#laws.0.45.0">1983 c 222 s 45</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.24"><b>273.24</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/222/#laws.0.45.0">1983 c 222 s 45</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.25">
        <h1 class="shn">273.25 LISTS TO BE VERIFIED.</h1>
        <p>Every person required to list property for taxation shall make out and deliver to the assessor, upon blanks furnished by the assessor, a verified statement of all personal property owned on January 2 of the current year. The person shall also make separate statements in like manner of all personal property possessed or controlled by the person and required by this chapter to be listed for taxation as agent or attorney, guardian, parent, trustee, executor, administrator, receiver, accounting officer, partner, factor, or in any other capacity; but no person shall be required to include in the statement any share of the capital stock of any company or corporation which it is required to list and return as its capital and property for taxation in this state.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.25.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2002</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 819</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/709/#laws.0.6.0">1969 c 709 s 6</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.26">
        <h1 class="shn">273.26 PERSONAL PROPERTY; WHERE LISTED.</h1>
        <p>Except as otherwise in this chapter provided, personal property shall be listed and assessed in the county, town, or district where the owner, agent, or trustee resides.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.26.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2003</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 820</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.27"><b>273.27</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/593/#laws.0.46.0">1984 c 593 s 46</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.28"><b>273.28</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/222/#laws.0.45.0">1983 c 222 s 45</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.29"><b>273.29</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/222/#laws.0.45.0">1983 c 222 s 45</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.30"><b>273.30</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/222/#laws.0.45.0">1983 c 222 s 45</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.31"><b>273.31</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/222/#laws.0.45.0">1983 c 222 s 45</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.32">
        <h1 class="shn">273.32 ELEVATORS AND WAREHOUSES ON RAILROAD.</h1>
        <p>All elevators and warehouses, with the machinery and fixtures therein, situated upon the land of any railroad company, which are not in good faith owned, operated, and exclusively controlled by such company, shall be listed and assessed as personal property in the town or district where situated, in the name of the owner, if known, and, if not known, as "owner unknown."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.32.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2008</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 825</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.33">
        <h1 class="shn">273.33 EXPRESS, STAGE AND TRANSPORTATION COMPANIES; PIPELINES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.33.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Listing and assessment in county.</span></h2>
          <p>The personal property of express, stage and transportation companies, and of pipeline companies engaged in the business of transporting products, except as otherwise provided by law, shall be listed and assessed in the county, town or district where the same is usually kept.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.33.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Listing and assessment by commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The personal property, consisting of the pipeline system of mains, pipes, and equipment attached thereto, of pipeline companies and others engaged in the operations or business of transporting products by pipelines, shall be listed with and assessed by the commissioner of revenue and the values provided to the city or county assessor by order. This subdivision shall not apply to the assessment of the products transported through the pipelines nor to the lines of local commercial gas companies engaged primarily in the business of distributing products to consumers at retail nor to pipelines used by the owner thereof to supply products exclusively for such owner's own consumption and not for resale to others. If more than 85 percent of the products actually transported over the pipeline is used for the owner's own consumption and not for resale to others, then this subdivision shall not apply; provided, however, that in that event, the pipeline shall be assessed in proportion to the percentage of products actually transported over such pipeline that is not used for the owner's own consumption. On or before August 1, the commissioner shall certify to the auditor of each county, the amount of such personal property assessment against each company in each district in which such property is located. If the commissioner determines that the amount of personal property assessment certified on or before August 1 is in error, the commissioner may issue a corrected certification on or before October 1. The commissioner may correct errors that are merely clerical in nature until December 31.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.33.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2009</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 826</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1943/0/604/#laws.0.1.0">1943 c 604 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/547/#laws.0.1.0">1949 c 547 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/582/#laws.0.3.0">1973 c 582 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/300/#laws.0.10.0">1985 c 300 s 10</a>,11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.7.35.0">1987 c 268 art 7 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.9.26.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 9 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/375/#laws.5.29.0">1993 c 375 art 5 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.13.31.0">2008 c 154 art 13 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/88/#laws.10.9.0">2009 c 88 art 10 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/112/#laws.3.5.0">2011 c 112 art 3 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.10.6.0">2014 c 308 art 10 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.15.18.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 15 s 18</a>,19</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.34"><b>273.34</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/222/#laws.0.45.0">1983 c 222 s 45</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.35">
        <h1 class="shn">273.35 GAS AND WATER COMPANIES.</h1>
        <p>The personal property of gas and water companies shall be listed and assessed in the town or district where located, without regard to where the principal or other place of business of the company may be located.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.35.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2011</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 828</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/449/#laws.0.1.0">1949 c 449 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.36">
        <h1 class="shn">273.36 ELECTRIC LIGHT AND POWER COMPANIES.</h1>
        <p>Personal property of electric light and power companies having a fixed situs in any city in this state shall be listed and assessed where situated, without regard to where the principal or other place of business of the company is located. Transmission lines having a voltage of 69 kv and above, all attachments and appurtenances thereto, having a fixed situs in this state, other than in an unorganized township, shall be listed and assessed where situated, without regard to where the principal or other place of business of the company is located.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.36.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2012</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1921/0/482/#laws.0.0.0">1921 c 482</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/123/#laws.5.7.0">1973 c 123 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/607/#laws.10.1.0">1980 c 607 art 10 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.37">
        <h1 class="shn">273.37 COMPANIES SUPPLYING ELECTRIC POWER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.37.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Listing and assessment where situated.</span></h2>
          <p>Personal property of electric light and power companies, and other individuals and partnerships supplying electric light and power, having a fixed situs outside of the corporate limits of cities shall be listed and assessed in the district where situated, except as otherwise provided.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.37.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Listing and assessment by commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Transmission lines of less than 69 kv, transmission lines of 69 kv and above located in an unorganized township, and distribution lines, and equipment attached thereto, having a fixed situs outside the corporate limits of cities except distribution lines taxed as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.40">273.40 </a>and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.41">273.41</a>, shall be listed with and assessed by the commissioner of revenue in the county where situated and the values provided to the city or county assessor by order. The commissioner shall assess such property at the percentage of market value fixed by law; and, on or before August 1, shall certify to the auditor of each county in which such property is located the amount of the assessment made against each company and person owning such property. If the commissioner determines that the amount of the assessment certified on or before August 1 is in error, the commissioner may issue a corrected certification on or before October 1. The commissioner may correct errors that are merely clerical in nature until December 31.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.37.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 2004 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.5.46.0">2005 c 151 art 5 s 46</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.37.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2012-1</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1925/0/306/#laws.0.1.0">1925 c 306 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/321/#laws.0.1.0">1939 c 321 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/554/#laws.0.1.0">1949 c 554 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/427/#laws.0.19.0">1971 c 427 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/123/#laws.5.7.0">1973 c 123 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/582/#laws.0.3.0">1973 c 582 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/607/#laws.10.2.0">1980 c 607 art 10 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.6.33.0">1987 c 268 art 6 s 33</a>; art 7 s 36; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.28.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.9.27.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 9 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/264/#laws.3.12.0">1995 c 264 art 3 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/490/#laws.5.15.0">2000 c 490 art 5 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.13.32.0">2008 c 154 art 13 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/88/#laws.10.10.0">2009 c 88 art 10 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/112/#laws.3.6.0">2011 c 112 art 3 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.10.7.0">2014 c 308 art 10 s 7</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.371">
        <h1 class="shn">273.371 REPORTS OF UTILITY COMPANIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.371.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Report required.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Every electric light, power, gas, water, express, stage, transportation, and pipeline company doing business in Minnesota shall annually file with the commissioner on or before March 31 a report under oath setting forth the information prescribed by the commissioner to enable the commissioner to make valuations, recommended valuations, and equalization required under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.33">273.33</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.35">273.35</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.36">273.36</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.37">273.37</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.3711">273.3711</a>. The commissioner shall prescribe the content, format, and manner of the report pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.30">270C.30</a>, except that for cooperative associations defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.40">273.40</a>, the information provided in the report must be aggregated to the unique taxing jurisdiction level and exclude information related to property subject to the in-lieu tax under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.41">273.41</a>, and that a "law administered by the commissioner" includes the property tax laws. If all the required information is not available on March 31, the company shall file the information that is available on or before March 31, and the balance of the information as soon as it becomes available. If a report is made by electronic means, the taxpayer's signature is defined pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.304">270C.304</a>, except that a "law administered by the commissioner" includes the property tax laws. For purposes of this subdivision, "unique taxing jurisdiction" means the geographic area subject to the same set of local tax rates.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.371.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Extension.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner for good cause may extend the time for filing the report required by subdivision 1. The extension must not exceed 15 days.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.371.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Reports filed by the commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>If a company fails to file a report required by subdivision 1, the commissioner may, from information in the commissioner's possession or obtainable by the commissioner, make and file a report for the company or make the valuations, recommended valuations, and equalizations required under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.33">273.33</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.35">273.35</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.37">273.37</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.3711">273.3711</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.371.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.9.28.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 9 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/604/#laws.3.21.0">1990 c 604 art 3 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.13.33.0">2008 c 154 art 13 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.16.21.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 16 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.4.18.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 4 s 18</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.3711">
        <h1 class="shn">273.3711 RECOMMENDED AND ORDERED VALUES.</h1>
        <p class="in">For purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.33">273.33</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.35">273.35</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.36">273.36</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.37">273.37</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.371">273.371</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.372">273.372</a>, all preliminary values not required to be listed and assessed by the commissioner of revenue are recommended values. If the commissioner provides preliminary recommended values, the values must be certified to the auditor of each county in which the property is located on or before July 15. If the commissioner determines that the certified recommended value is in error the commissioner may issue a corrected certification on or before October 1. The commissioner may correct errors that are merely clerical in nature until December 31.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.3711.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/154/#laws.13.34.0">2008 c 154 art 13 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/112/#laws.3.7.0">2011 c 112 art 3 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.10.8.0">2014 c 308 art 10 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.4.19.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 4 s 19</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.372">
        <h1 class="shn">273.372 PROCEEDINGS AND APPEALS; UTILITY OR RAILROAD VALUATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.372.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) As provided in this section, an appeal by a utility or railroad company concerning property for which the commissioner of revenue has provided the city or county assessor with valuations by order, or for which the commissioner has recommended values to the city or county assessor, must be brought against the commissioner, and not against the county or taxing district where the property is located.</p>
          <p>(b) This section governs administrative appeals and appeals to court of a claim that utility or railroad operating property has been partially, unfairly, or unequally assessed, or assessed at a valuation greater than its real or actual value, misclassified, or that the property is exempt. This section applies only to property described in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270.81#stat.270.81.1">270.81, subdivision 1</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.33">273.33</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.35">273.35</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.36">273.36</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.37">273.37</a>, and only with regard to taxable net tax capacities that have been provided to the city or county by the commissioner and which have not been changed by city or county. If the taxable net tax capacity being appealed is not the taxable net tax capacity established by the commissioner, or if the appeal claims that the tax rate applied against the parcel is incorrect, or that the tax has been paid, this section does not apply.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.372.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Contents and filing of petition.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) In all appeals to court that are required to be brought against the commissioner under this section, the petition initiating the appeal must be served on the commissioner and must be filed with the Tax Court in Ramsey County, as provided in paragraph (b) or (c).</p>
          <p>(b) If the appeal to court is from an order of the commissioner, it must be brought under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/271">271</a> and filed within the time period prescribed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/271.06#stat.271.06.2">271.06, subdivision 2</a>, except that when the provisions of this section conflict with chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/271">271</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/278">278</a>, this section prevails. In addition, the petition must include all the parcels encompassed by that order which the petitioner claims have been partially, unfairly, or unequally assessed, assessed at a valuation greater than their real or actual value, misclassified, or are exempt. For this purpose, an order of the commissioner is either (1) a certification or notice of value by the commissioner for property described in subdivision 1, or (2) the final determination by the commissioner of an administrative appeal described in subdivision 4.</p>
          <p>(c) If the appeal is from the tax that results from implementation of the commissioner's order, certification, or recommendation, it must be brought under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/278">278</a>, and the provisions in that chapter apply, except that service shall be on the commissioner only and not on the local officials specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/278.01#stat.278.01.1">278.01, subdivision 1</a>, and if any other provision of this section conflicts with chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/278">278</a>, this section prevails. In addition, the petition must include either all the utility parcels or all the railroad parcels in the state in which the petitioner claims an interest and which the petitioner claims have been partially, unfairly, or unequally assessed, assessed at a valuation greater than their real or actual value, misclassified, or are exempt.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.372.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Notice.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon filing of any appeal in court by a utility company or railroad against the commissioner pursuant to this section, the commissioner shall give notice by first class mail to the county auditor of each county where property included in the petition is located.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.372.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Administrative appeals.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Companies that submit the reports under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270.82">270.82</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.371">273.371</a> by the date specified in that section, or by the date specified by the commissioner in an extension, may appeal administratively to the commissioner prior to bringing an action in court.</p>
          <p>(b) Companies must file a written request for an appeal with the commissioner within 30 days after the notice date of the commissioner's valuation certification or other notice to the company. For purposes of this section, "notice date" means the notice date of the valuation certification, commissioner's order, recommendation, or other notice.</p>
          <p>(c) The appeal need not be in any particular form but must contain the following information:</p>
          <p>(1) name and address of the company;</p>
          <p>(2) the date;</p>
          <p>(3) its Minnesota identification number;</p>
          <p>(4) the assessment year or period involved;</p>
          <p>(5) the findings in the valuation that the company disputes;</p>
          <p>(6) a summary statement specifying its reasons for disputing each item; and</p>
          <p>(7) the signature of the company's duly authorized agent or representative.</p>
          <p>(d) When requested in writing and within the time allowed for filing an administrative appeal, the commissioner may extend the time for filing an appeal for a period of not more than 15 days from the expiration of the time for filing the appeal.</p>
          <p>(e) The commissioner shall conduct the conference either in person or by telephone upon the commissioner's entire files and records and such further information as may be offered. The conference must be held no later than 20 days after the date of the request for an appeal. Within 30 days after the conference the commissioner shall make a final determination of the matter and shall notify the company promptly of the determination. The conference is not a contested case hearing subject to chapter 14.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.372.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Agreement determining valuation.</span></h2>
          <p>When it appears to be in the best interest of the state, the commissioner may settle any matter under consideration regarding an appeal filed under this section. The agreement must be in writing and signed by the commissioner and the company or the company's authorized representative. The agreement is final and conclusive, and except upon a showing of fraud, malfeasance, or misrepresentation of a material fact, the case may not be reopened as to the matters agreed upon.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.372.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Dismissal of administrative appeal.</span></h2>
          <p>If a taxpayer files an administrative appeal from an order of the commissioner and also files an appeal to the Tax Court for that same order of the commissioner, the administrative appeal is dismissed and the commissioner is no longer required to make the determination of appeal under subdivision 4.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.372.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/490/#laws.5.16.0">2000 c 490 art 5 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.5.20.0">2003 c 127 art 5 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.5.24.0">2005 c 151 art 5 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/143/#laws.17.11.0">2013 c 143 art 17 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.15.20.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 15 s 20</a>-23; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/182/#laws.1.71.0">2018 c 182 art 1 s 71</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.38">
        <h1 class="shn">273.38 PERCENTAGE OF ASSESSMENTS; EXCEPTIONS.</h1>
        <p>The distribution lines and the attachments and appurtenances thereto of cooperative associations organized under the provisions of Laws 1923, chapter 326, and laws amendatory thereof and supplemental thereto, and engaged in the electrical heat, light and power business, upon a mutual, nonprofit and cooperative plan, shall be assessed and taxed as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.40">273.40</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.41">273.41</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.38.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2012-2</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1925/0/306/#laws.0.2.0">1925 c 306 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/321/#laws.0.2.0">1939 c 321 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/554/#laws.0.2.0">1949 c 554 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/427/#laws.0.20.0">1971 c 427 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/582/#laws.0.3.0">1973 c 582 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1974/0/47/#laws.0.1.0">1974 c 47 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/14/#laws.4.69.0">1Sp1985 c 14 art 4 s 69</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.6.34.0">1987 c 268 art 6 s 34</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.39">
        <h1 class="shn">273.39 RURAL AREA.</h1>
        <p class="in">As used in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.39">273.39</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.41">273.41</a>, the term "rural area" shall be deemed to mean any area of the state not included within the boundaries of any statutory city or home rule charter city, and such term shall be deemed to include both farm and nonfarm population thereof.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.39.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2012-5</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/303/#laws.0.2.0">1939 c 303 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/123/#laws.5.7.0">1973 c 123 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/143/#laws.17.12.0">2013 c 143 art 17 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.40">
        <h1 class="shn">273.40 ANNUAL TAX ON COOPERATIVE ASSOCIATIONS.</h1>
        <p>Cooperative associations organized under the provisions of Laws 1923, chapter 326, and laws amendatory thereof and laws supplemental thereto, and engaged in electrical heat, light, or power business upon a mutual, nonprofit, and cooperative plan in rural areas, as hereinafter defined, are hereby recognized as quasi-public in their nature and purposes; but such cooperative associations, which operate within the corporate limits of any city shall have a tax capacity of the market value of that portion of its property located within the corporate limits of any city as provided for in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a>, subdivisions 24 and 31.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.40.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2012-4</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/303/#laws.0.1.0">1939 c 303 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1943/0/643/#laws.0.2.0">1943 c 643 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/427/#laws.0.21.0">1971 c 427 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/123/#laws.5.7.0">1973 c 123 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/1/1/#laws.8.8.0">1Sp1981 c 1 art 8 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.29.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 29</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.41">
        <h1 class="shn">273.41 AMOUNT OF TAX; DISTRIBUTION.</h1>
        <p>There is hereby imposed upon each such cooperative association on December 31 of each year a tax of $10 for each 100 members, or fraction thereof, of such association. The tax, when paid, shall be in lieu of all personal property taxes, state, county, or local, upon distribution lines and the attachments and appurtenances thereto of such associations located in rural areas. The tax shall be payable on or before March 1 of the next succeeding year, to the commissioner of revenue. If the tax, or any portion thereof, is not paid within the time herein specified for the payment thereof, there shall be added thereto a specific penalty equal to ten percent of the amount so remaining unpaid. Such penalty shall be collected as part of said tax, and the amount of said tax not timely paid, together with said penalty, shall bear interest at the rate specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270C.40">270C.40</a> from the time such tax should have been paid until paid. The commissioner shall deposit the amount so received in the general fund of the state treasury.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.41.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1939/0/303/#laws.0.3.0">1939 c 303 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1951/0/590/#laws.0.1.0">1951 c 590 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/0/158/#laws.0.18.0">1959 c 158 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/1/31/#laws.20.7.0">Ex1971 c 31 art 20 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/582/#laws.0.3.0">1973 c 582 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/650/#laws.3.1.0">1973 c 650 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/377/#laws.0.8.0">1975 c 377 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/151/#laws.2.17.0">2005 c 151 art 2 s 17</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.42">
        <h1 class="shn">273.42 RATE OF TAX; ENTRY AND CERTIFICATION; CREDIT ON PAYMENT; PROPERTY TAX CREDIT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.42.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Tax rate; payment.</span></h2>
          <p>The property set forth in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.37#stat.273.37.2">273.37, subdivision 2</a>, consisting of transmission lines of less than 69 kv and transmission lines of 69 kv and above located in an unorganized township, and distribution lines not taxed as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.38">273.38</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.40">273.40</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.41">273.41</a> shall be taxed at the average local tax rate of taxes levied for all purposes throughout the county after disparity reduction aid is applied, and shall be entered on the tax lists by the county auditor against the owner thereof and certified to the county treasurer at the same time and in the same manner that other taxes are certified, and, when paid, shall be credited as follows: 50 percent to the general revenue fund of the county and 50 percent to the general school fund of the county, except that if there are high voltage transmission lines as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/216E.01">216E.01</a>, the construction of which was commenced after July 1, 1974, and which are located in unorganized townships within the county, then the distribution of taxes within this subdivision shall be credited as follows: 50 percent to the general revenue fund of the county, 40 percent to the general school fund of the county and ten percent to a utility property tax credit fund, which is hereby established.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.42.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Property tax credit.</span></h2>
          <p>Owners of land that is an agricultural or nonagricultural homestead, nonhomestead agricultural land, rental residential property, and both commercial and noncommercial seasonal residential recreational property, as those terms are defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.13">273.13</a> listed on records of the county auditor or county treasurer over which runs a high voltage transmission line with a capacity of 200 kilovolts or more, except a high voltage transmission line the construction of which was commenced prior to July 1, 1974, shall receive a property tax credit in an amount determined by multiplying a fraction, the numerator of which is the length of high voltage transmission line which runs over that parcel and the denominator of which is the total length of that particular line running over all property within the city or township by ten percent of the transmission line tax revenue derived from the tax on that portion of the line within the city or township pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.36">273.36</a>. In the case of property owners in unorganized townships, the property tax credit shall be determined by multiplying a fraction, the numerator of which is the length of the qualifying high voltage transmission line which runs over the parcel and the denominator of which is the total length of the qualifying high voltage transmission line running over all property within all the unorganized townships within the county, by the total utility property tax credit fund amount available within the county for that year pursuant to subdivision 1. Where a right-of-way width is shared by more than one property owner, the numerator shall be adjusted by multiplying the length of line on the parcel by the proportion of the total width on the parcel owned by that property owner. The amount of credit for which the property qualifies shall not exceed 20 percent of the total gross tax on the parcel prior to deduction of the state paid agricultural credit, provided that, if the property containing the right-of-way is included in a parcel which exceeds 40 acres, the total gross tax on the parcel shall be multiplied by a fraction, the numerator of which is the sum of the number of acres in each quarter-quarter section or portion thereof which contains a right-of-way and the denominator of which is the total number of acres in the parcel set forth on the tax statement, and the maximum credit shall be 20 percent of the product of that computation, prior to deduction of those credits. The auditor of the county in which the affected parcel is located shall calculate the amount of the credit due for each parcel and transmit that information to the county treasurer. The county auditor, in computing the credit received pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.135">273.135</a>, shall reduce the gross tax by the amount of the credit received pursuant to this section, unless the amount of the credit would be less than $10.</p>
          <p>If, after the county auditor has computed the credit to those qualifying property owners in unorganized townships, there is money remaining in the utility property tax credit fund, then that excess amount in the fund shall be returned to the general school fund of the county.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.273.42.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">State tax on transmission and distribution lines.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.425">273.425</a>, the entire tax capacity of property taxed at the average local tax rate under subdivision 1 is subject to the state tax rate provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/275.025">275.025</a>. Notwithstanding subdivisions 1 and 2, the entire proceeds of the state tax levy for each such property must be distributed to the state under the procedures provided in chapter 276. No portion of the proceeds from the state levy on such property is distributed within the county under subdivision 1 or 2.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.42.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2012-3</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1925/0/306/#laws.0.3.0">1925 c 306 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1949/0/554/#laws.0.3.0">1949 c 554 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/658/#laws.0.4.0">1978 c 658 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/303/#laws.2.20.0">1979 c 303 art 2 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/607/#laws.10.3.0">1980 c 607 art 10 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/1/1/#laws.2.15.0">1Sp1981 c 1 art 2 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/523/#laws.16.1.0">1982 c 523 art 16 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/14/#laws.4.70.0">1Sp1985 c 14 art 4 s 70</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/1/1/#laws.4.24.0">1Sp1986 c 1 art 4 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/268/#laws.6.35.0">1987 c 268 art 6 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.2.11.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 2 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/604/#laws.3.22.0">1990 c 604 art 3 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/5/#laws.3.44.0">1Sp2001 c 5 art 3 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.5.21.0">2003 c 127 art 5 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/275/#laws.1.90.0">2014 c 275 art 1 s 90</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.425">
        <h1 class="shn">273.425 ADJUSTMENT OF LEVY.</h1>
        <p>When preparing tax lists pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/275.28">275.28</a> for each levy year for which credits will be payable under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.42">273.42</a>, the county auditor shall deduct from the net tax capacity of the property within the county an amount equal to ten percent of the net tax capacity of transmission lines with respect to which a credit is to be paid and which are valued pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.36">273.36</a>. The local tax rate necessary to be applied to this reduced total net tax capacity in order to raise the required amount of tax revenue for the local taxing authorities shall be applied to the net tax capacity of all taxable property in the county, including the entire net tax capacity of those transmission lines. The proceeds of the tax levied against the excluded ten percent of the net tax capacity of those transmission lines shall be available for purposes of funding of the credit provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/273.42">273.42</a>. If the amount of that portion of the levy exceeds the amount necessary to fund the credits, the excess shall be distributed to the taxing districts within which the affected property is located in proportion to their respective local tax rates, to be used for general levy purposes.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.425.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/303/#laws.2.21.0">1979 c 303 art 2 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/523/#laws.16.2.0">1982 c 523 art 16 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/719/#laws.5.84.0">1988 c 719 art 5 s 84</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/329/#laws.13.20.0">1989 c 329 art 13 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/1/1/#laws.2.11.0">1Sp1989 c 1 art 2 s 11</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.43">
        <h1 class="shn">273.43 PERSONAL PROPERTY OF CERTAIN COMPANIES, WHERE LISTED.</h1>
        <p>The personal property of street railroad, street railway, plank road, gravel road, turnpike, or bridge companies shall be listed in the county, town, city, or district where such property is situated, and where such personal property is situated in different counties, towns, cities, or districts, such part of such personal property situated in such county, town, city, or district, shall be listed and assessed by the commissioner of revenue in the taxing district where the same is situated, without regard to where the principal or any other place of business of such company is located.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.43.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2013</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 829</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1913/0/25/#laws.0.1.0">1913 c 25 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/123/#laws.5.7.0">1973 c 123 art 5 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/582/#laws.0.3.0">1973 c 582 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.44"><b>273.44</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/222/#laws.0.45.0">1983 c 222 s 45</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.45"><b>273.45</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/222/#laws.0.45.0">1983 c 222 s 45</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.46">
        <h1 class="shn">273.46 ASSIGNEES AND RECEIVERS.</h1>
        <p>Personal property in the hands of an assignee or receiver shall be listed and assessed at the place of listing before the appointment of the assignee or receiver.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.46.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2016</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 832</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.47">
        <h1 class="shn">273.47 PROPERTY MOVED BETWEEN JANUARY AND MARCH.</h1>
        <p>The owner of personal property, removing from one county, town, or district to another between January 2 and March 1, shall be assessed in either in which the owner is first called upon by the assessor. A person moving into this state from another state between those dates shall list the property the person owns on January 2 of such year in the county, town, or district in which the person resides, unless it appears to the assessor that the person is held for tax of the current year on the property in another state.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.47.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2017</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 833</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/709/#laws.0.8.0">1969 c 709 s 8</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.48">
        <h1 class="shn">273.48 WHERE LISTED IN CASE OF DOUBT.</h1>
        <p>In case of doubt as to the proper place of listing personal property, or where it cannot be listed as in this chapter provided, if between places in the same county, the place for listing and assessing shall be determined by the county board of equalization; and, if between different counties, or places in different counties, by the commissioner of revenue; and when determined in either case shall be as binding as if fixed hereby.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.48.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2018</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 834</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1911/0/223/#laws.0.1.0">1911 c 223 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/582/#laws.0.3.0">1973 c 582 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.49"><b>273.49</b> MS 1992 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/375/#laws.3.47.0">1993 c 375 art 3 s 47</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.50">
        <h1 class="shn">273.50 LISTS MAY BE DESTROYED.</h1>
        <p>The county auditor may destroy any list or statement of personal property on file in the auditor's office after the expiration of six years from the date when the taxes thereon have been paid or become delinquent. If any proceeding has been begun to enforce payment of such taxes, such list or statement shall not be destroyed before the expiration of one year from the return of an execution unsatisfied, or the termination of the proceeding.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.50.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2020</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 837</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.51"><b>273.51</b> MS 1945 [Impliedly repealed, see Bemis Bro Bag Co v Wallace, 197 Minn 216, 266 NW 690]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.52"><b>273.52</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/222/#laws.0.45.0">1983 c 222 s 45</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.53"><b>273.53</b> MS 1967 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.99.0">1969 c 9 s 99</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.54"><b>273.54</b> MS 1967 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.99.0">1969 c 9 s 99</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.55"><b>273.55</b> MS 1967 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.99.0">1969 c 9 s 99</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.56"><b>273.56</b> MS 1982 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/593/#laws.0.46.0">1984 c 593 s 46</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.57"><b>273.57</b> MS 1967 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.99.0">1969 c 9 s 99</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.58"><b>273.58</b> MS 1967 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.99.0">1969 c 9 s 99</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.59"><b>273.59</b> MS 1967 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.99.0">1969 c 9 s 99</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.60"><b>273.60</b> MS 1967 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.99.0">1969 c 9 s 99</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.61"><b>273.61</b> MS 1967 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.99.0">1969 c 9 s 99</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.62"><b>273.62</b> MS 1967 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.99.0">1969 c 9 s 99</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.63"><b>273.63</b> MS 1967 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.99.0">1969 c 9 s 99</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.64"><b>273.64</b> MS 1967 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/9/#laws.0.99.0">1969 c 9 s 99</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.65">
