        <h1 class="shn">273.65 FAILURE TO LIST; EXAMINATION UNDER OATH; DUTIES OF ASSESSOR.</h1>
        <p>When the assessor shall be of opinion that the person listing property for that person, or for any other person, company, or corporation, has not made a full, fair, and complete list thereof, the assessor may examine such person, under oath, in regard to the amount of the property required to be listed; and, if such person shall refuse to make full discovery under oath, the assessor may list the property of such person, or the person's principal, according to the assessor's best judgment and information.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.65.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2030</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 843</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.66">
        <h1 class="shn">273.66 OWNER ABSENT OR SICK.</h1>
        <p>If any person required to list property be sick or absent when the assessor calls for a list thereof, the assessor shall leave at the office or usual place of residence or business of such person a written or printed notice requiring such person to make out and leave at a place, and on or before a day named therein, the statement or list required by this chapter. The date of leaving such notice, and the name of the person so required to list, shall be noted by the assessor in the assessment book.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.66.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2031</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 844</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.67">
        <h1 class="shn">273.67 PROCEDURE WHEN OWNER DOES NOT LIST OR IS NOT SWORN.</h1>
        <p>When any person whose duty it is to list shall refuse or neglect to list personal property when called on by the assessor, or to take and subscribe the required oath in regard to the truth of a statement, or any part thereof, the assessor shall enter opposite the name of such person, in an appropriate column, the words "refused to list," or "refused to swear," as the case may be; and when any person whose duty it is to list is absent, or unable from sickness to list, the assessor shall enter opposite the name of such person, in an appropriate column, the word "absent" or "sick." The assessor may administer oaths to all persons who by this chapter are required to swear, or whom the assessor may require to testify, and may examine, upon oath, any person supposed to have knowledge of the amount or value of the personal property of any person refusing to list or to verify a list of personal property.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.67.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2032</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 845</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.68">
        <h1 class="shn">273.68 FAILURE TO OBTAIN LIST.</h1>
        <p>In case of failure to obtain a statement of personal property, the assessor shall ascertain the amount and value of such property, and assess the same at such amount as the assessor believes to be the market value thereof. When requested, the assessor shall sign and deliver to the person assessed a copy of the statement showing the valuation of the property so listed.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.68.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/11">2033</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1905/cite/11">RL s 846</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/339/#laws.0.8.0">1975 c 339 s 8</a>; 1986 c 444</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.69"><b>273.69</b> MS 1969 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/1/31/#laws.31.1.0">Ex1971 c 31 art 31 s 1</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.70"><b>273.70</b> MS 1969 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/1/31/#laws.31.1.0">Ex1971 c 31 art 31 s 1</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.71"><b>273.71</b> MS 1986 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.244.0">1987 c 291 s 244</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.72"><b>273.72</b> MS 1986 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.244.0">1987 c 291 s 244</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.73"><b>273.73</b> MS 1986 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.244.0">1987 c 291 s 244</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.74"><b>273.74</b> MS 1986 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.244.0">1987 c 291 s 244</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.75"><b>273.75</b> MS 1986 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.244.0">1987 c 291 s 244</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.76"><b>273.76</b> MS 1986 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.244.0">1987 c 291 s 244</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.77"><b>273.77</b> MS 1986 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.244.0">1987 c 291 s 244</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.78"><b>273.78</b> MS 1986 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.244.0">1987 c 291 s 244</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.80"><b>273.80</b> MS 2012 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/308/#laws.9.94.0">2014 c 308 art 9 s 94</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.273.86"><b>273.86</b> MS 1986 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/291/#laws.0.244.0">1987 c 291 s 244</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.273.88">
        <h1 class="shn">273.88 EQUALIZATION OF PUBLIC UTILITY STRUCTURES.</h1>
        <p>After making the apportionment provided in Minnesota Rules, part <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/8100.0600">8100.0600</a>, the commissioner must equalize the values of the operating structures to the level accepted by the State Board of Equalization if the appropriate sales ratio for each county, as conducted by the Department of Revenue pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/270.12#stat.270.12.2">270.12, subdivision 2</a>, clause (6), is outside the range accepted by the State Board of Equalization. The commissioner must not equalize the value of the operating structures if the sales ratio determined pursuant to this subdivision is within the range accepted by the State Board of Equalization.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.273.88.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/1/1/#laws.15.24.0">1Sp2017 c 1 art 15 s 24</a>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
